> DEVELOPER

Lane B: 持久化层 — storage adapter + 启动加载

  ## 背景
  所有 feature 的 state 都是 JS 内存闭包，关闭应用数据全丢。需要接入真实文件持久化。
  storage-local-baseline feature 的 service 层已 100% 完成，只缺真实 adapter。
  platform/files.ts 的 FileFacade 已实现（readTextFile/writeTextFile），但无消费者。

  ## 直接合同（按顺序读取）
  1. rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/ — storage feature 全部文件（service/state/adapters/ports）
  2. rewrite/src/platform/files.ts — FileFacade 接口
  3. rewrite/src/platform/index.ts — platform facade
  4. rewrite/src/runtime/feature-wiring.ts — 看 storage 是怎么接的（当前传空数组）
  5. rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts — runtime 启动流程
  6. rewrite/src/app/AppShell.vue — 应用启动入口
  7. codestable/features/rewrite-storage-local-baseline/ — storage 设计文档（如果有）

  ## 任务

  ### 第一步：实现 RealLocalMaterialAdapter
  基于 storage-local-baseline 的 adapter port（LocalMaterialAdapter），
  实现一个通过 FileFacade 读写 JSON 文件的真实 adapter。
  - 数据目录：通过 Electron dialog 或固定路径获取
  - 文件格式：JSON
  - 错误处理：文件不存在时返回空、损坏时 log + 返回空

  ### 第二步：接入 runtime wiring
  修改 feature-wiring.ts：
  - 用 RealLocalMaterialAdapter 替换当前传空数组的 createDefaultStorageReader()
  - 检查 platform facade 是否可用（renderer 侧），不可用时 fallback 到 fake

  ### 第三步：启动时加载持久化数据
  在 AppShell 或 runtime bootstrap 中：
  - 启动时从 storage 加载帧定义、连接配置等基础数据
  - 注入到对应 feature 的 state 中
  - 如果没有持久化数据，加载默认模板（public/data/templates/ 下的 JSON）

  ### 第四步：关键节点自动保存
  在以下操作后触发持久化：
  - 帧定义增删改
  - 连接配置保存
  - 任务模板保存

  ## 验证
  1. pnpm -C rewrite build
  2. pnpm -C rewrite lint
  3. pnpm -C rewrite test --run
  4. 启动 dev → 创建帧定义 → 关闭 → 重新打开 → 确认帧定义还在

  ## 完成后提交

49	    readonly message: string;
50	  }[];
51	}
52	
53	type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
54	type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
55	type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
56	  StorageLocalReader,
57	  | 'getLastIssue'
58	  | 'listCsvMaterials'
59	  | 'listHistoryHours'
60	  | 'listLegacyMaterials'
61	  | 'listLocalRecords'
62	>;
63	
64	export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
65	
66	export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
67	  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
68	  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
69	  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
70	  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
71	}
72	
73	export interface RewriteRuntime {
74	  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
75	  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
76	  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
77	  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;
78	  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;
79	  stopTickDriver(): void;
80	  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;
81	  destroy(): void;
82	}
83	
84	function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
85	  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
86	  return {
87	    connect: async () => accepted,
88	    disconnect: async () => accepted,
89	    write: async () => accepted,
90	    cleanup: async () => accepted,
91	    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
96	  return {
97	    ok: result.ok,
98	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
99	      severity: issue.severity,
100	      code: issue.code,
101	      message: issue.message,
102	    })),
103	  };
104	}
105	
106	export function createRewriteRuntime(
107	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
108	): RewriteRuntime {
109	  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
110	  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
111	
112	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
113	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
114	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
115	
116	  let destroyed = false;
117	  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
118	
119	  function stopTick(): void {
120	    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
121	      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
122	      tickIntervalId = null;
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  return {
127	    features: wiredFeatures,
128	
129	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
130	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
131	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
132	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
133	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
134	
135	      return {
136	        frame: {
137	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
138	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
139	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
140	        },
141	        settings: {
142	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
143	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
144	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
145	        },
146	        storage: {
147	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
148	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
149	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
150	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
151	          lastIssue: storageIssue
152	            ? {
153	                code: storageIssue.code,
154	                message: storageIssue.message,
155	              }
156	            : null,
157	        },
158	      };
159	    },
160	
161	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
162	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
163	    },
164	
165	    routingTick() {
166	      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
167	    },
168	
169	    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
170	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
172	        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
173	      }, intervalMs);
174	    },
175	
176	    stopTickDriver() {
177	      stopTick();
178	    },
179	
180	    get isTickDriverRunning() {
181	      return tickIntervalId !== null;
182	    },
183	
184	    destroy() {
185	      if (destroyed) return;
186	      destroyed = true;
187	      stopTick();
188	      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();
189	      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
190	      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
191	    },
192	  };
193	}
194

58	function createDefaultFrameService(): FrameAssetService {
59	  return createFrameAssetService();
60	}
61	
62	function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
63	  return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
64	    records: [],
65	    historyMaterials: [],
66	    csvMaterials: [],
67	    legacyMaterials: [],
68	  }));
69	}

71	export function wireFeatures(
72	  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
73	): RewriteWiredFeatures {
74	  // L0: no cross-dependencies
75	  const frameService = createDefaultFrameService();
76	  const frameReader = frameService;
77	  const settingsService = createSettingsService();
78	  const storageReader = createDefaultStorageReader();
79	
80	  // L1: needs adapter
81	  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
82	    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
83	  });
84	
85	  // L2: needs L0 + L1
86	  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
87	
88	  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
89	  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
90	  const sendService = createSendService({
91	    frameReader,
92	    targetResolver,
93	    transportWriter: sendWriter,
94	  });
95	
96	  // L3: needs L2
97	  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
98	  const taskService = createTaskService({
99	    sendService,
100	    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
101	  });
102	
103	  // L4: needs L3 + config
104	  const defaultGlobalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig = {
105	    scoeIdentifier: '',
106	    tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',
107	    tcpServerPort: 0,
108	    tcpServerAutoConnect: false,
109	    udpIpAddress: '0.0.0.0',

114	    destinationIdentifierOffset: 0,

117	    functionCodeOffset: 0,
118	  };

130	    platformFileReader: async () => [],
131	    stateReader: commandIngressState.reader,
132	    stateWriter: commandIngressState.writer,
133	  });
134

32	  }
33	}
34	
35	function getBridge(): RewritePlatformBridge | undefined {
36	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
37	    return undefined;
38	  }
39	  return window[REWRITE_PLATFORM_BRIDGE_KEY];
40	}
41	
42	export function getRewritePlatformBridgeInfo(): RewritePlatformBridgeInfo | null {
43	  return getBridge()?.getBridgeInfo() ?? null;
44	}
45	
46	let cachedTransportFacade: TransportFacade | null = null;
47	
48	export function getTransportFacade(): TransportFacade | null {
49	  if (cachedTransportFacade) return cachedTransportFacade;
50	  const bridge = getBridge();
51	  if (!bridge?.transport) return null;
52	  cachedTransportFacade = createTransportFacade(bridge.transport as TransportBridge);
53	  return cachedTransportFacade;
54	}
55	
56	export function resetTransportFacade(): void {
57	  cachedTransportFacade = null;
58	}
59	
60	let cachedFileFacade: FileFacade | null = null;
61	
62	export function getFileFacade(): FileFacade | null {
63	  if (cachedFileFacade) return cachedFileFacade;
64	  const bridge = getBridge();
65	  if (!bridge?.file) return null;
66	  cachedFileFacade = createFileFacade(bridge.file as FileBridge);
67	  return cachedFileFacade;
68	}
69	
70	export function resetFileFacade(): void {
71	  cachedFileFacade = null;
72	}

Perfect! I've now read all files under the `rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/` directory. Let me provide you with a comprehensive overview of the storage feature.

## Complete Storage-Local-Baseline Feature Overview

### Directory Structure
The storage-local-baseline feature has a well-organized structure with 20 files across 7 directories:

```
storage-local-baseline/
├── core/               # Core business logic and types
├── state/              # State management
├── selectors/          # Data selectors
├── adapters/           # Adapter interfaces and implementations
├── services/           # Service layer
├── fixtures/           # Test fixtures
└── __tests__/          # Test files
```

### File-by-File Breakdown

#### Root Level (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/`)

**index.ts** (1)
- Public API entry point
- Exports: Types, services, and operations from core, adapters, and services
- Intentionally excludes internal state and fake adapters from public API

---

#### Core Directory (`core/`)

**index.ts** (7) 
- Re-exports all core functionality
- Modules: types, clone, validation, history, csv, legacy

**types.ts** (70 lines)
- Core type definitions for the entire storage system
- Key types:
  - `StorageLocalRecord` - Main record structure with id, capturedAt, source, channel, fields
  - `StorageRecordField` - Field structure with key, value, unit
  - `StorageHistoryMaterial` - Hour-grouped records
  - `StorageCsvMaterial` - CSV export structure
  - `StorageLegacyJsonMaterial` - Legacy material metadata
  - Validation types: `StorageValidationIssue`, `StorageValidationResult`

**clone.ts** (49 lines)
- Deep cloning utilities using `globalThis.structuredClone`
- Functions for cloning records, materials, and collections
- Ensures immutability across the system

**validation.ts** (236 lines)
- Normalization and validation for storage records
- Key functions:
  - `normalizeStorageLocalRecord()` - Validates and normalizes single records
  - `normalizeStorageLocalRecords()` - Validates record arrays
  - Field validation with duplicate key detection
  - Time validation, source validation
- Creates structured validation issues with location tracking

**history.ts** (182 lines)
- Time-based operations and record management
- Key functions:
  - `getStorageHourKey()` - Extracts hour key from timestamps
  - `sortStorageLocalRecords()` - Deterministic sorting by time then ID
  - `mergeStorageLocalRecords()` - Deduplicates by ID
  - `queryStorageLocalRecords()` - Filters by time, channel, fields
  - `cleanupStorageLocalRecords()` - Removes records before threshold
  - `summarizeStorageHours()` - Creates hour-level summaries

**csv.ts** (147 lines)
- CSV generation from storage records
- Key functions:
  - `createStorageCsvMaterial()` - Creates CSV with validation
  - `renderCsvRows()` - Renders CSV with proper escaping
  - Handles quotes, commas, newlines in values
  - Supports custom columns and headers

**legacy.ts** (55 lines)
- Legacy material handling
- `classifyStorageLegacyJsonMaterial()` - Validates legacy material metadata
- Only accepts array-shaped legacy materials

#### State Directory (`state/`)

- Functions:

- All operations return new snapshots (immutable updates)

- Query functions for state snapshots

  - `selectStorageCsvMaterialSummaries()` - List CSV metadata
  - `selectStorageCsvMaterial()` - Get specific CSV with cloning
  - `selectStorageLegacyMaterialSummaries()` - List legacy metadata
  - `selectStorageLegacyMaterial()` - Get specific legacy material
  - `selectStorageLastIssue()` - Get last error
- Returns cloned data to prevent mutation

  - `writeMaterial()` - Write to storage buckets
  - `deleteMaterial()` - Delete from storage buckets
  - `listMaterials()` - List items in buckets
- Result types for each operation (success/failure)
- Error types: cancelled, permission-denied, unavailable, corrupted, write-failed, missing

  - `appendLocalRecords()` - Add new records with validation
  - `loadHistoryMaterials()` - Load and merge history materials
  - `createCsvFromLocalRecords()` - Generate CSV from queried records
  - `saveLegacyJsonMaterial()` - Save legacy material
  - `copySnapshot()` - Save state snapshot
- Error handling converts adapter errors to validation issues

**storage-core-oracle.spec.ts** (87 lines)

**storage-fake-adapter.spec.ts** (58 lines)

**storage-service-state-selector.spec.ts** (159 lines)
- Integration tests for service, state, and selector
- Tests:
  - Append records through adapter with state isolation
  - Validation rejection without data changes
  - Adapter write failure handling
  - History material loading with summaries
  - CSV creation and snapshot copying
  - Legacy material saving with metadata
  - Public API doesn't expose internals

---

### Key Design Patterns

1. **Immutability**: All data is cloned using `structuredClone` to prevent external mutations
2. **Validation Pipeline**: Multi-stage validation with detailed error locations and codes
3. **Adapter Pattern**: Clean separation between business logic and storage implementation
4. **State Snapshots**: Immutable state with functional updates
5. **Service Layer**: Coordinates adapters, state, and selectors with proper error handling
6. **Test Isolation**: Public API excludes test utilities, test doubles for development
7. **Type Safety**: Comprehensive TypeScript types with readonly modifiers

This is a well-architected storage feature with clear separation of concerns, comprehensive error handling, and excellent test coverage. The design supports multiple storage backends through adapters while maintaining data integrity through validation and immutable state management.

1	import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';
2	import { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';
3	import { getTransportFacade } from '@/platform';
4	import { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';
5	
6	const rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');
7	
8	export interface BootstrapResult {
9	  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;
10	  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';
11	}
12	
13	export function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): BootstrapResult {
14	  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();
15	  if (transportFacade) {
16	    const adapter = createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade });
17	    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
18	    return { runtime, mode: 'real' };
19	  }
20	
21	  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime();
22	  return { runtime, mode: 'noOp' };
23	}
24	
25	export function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {
26	  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);
27	  return runtime;
28	}
29	
30	export function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {
31	  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);
32	  if (!runtime) {
33	    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');
34	  }
35	
36	  return runtime;
37	}
38

1	import {
2	  classifyStorageLegacyJsonMaterial,
3	  createStorageCsvMaterial,

11	  queryStorageLocalRecords,

13	  type StorageCsvMaterial,

15	  type StorageHourSummary,
16	  type StorageLegacyJsonMaterial,
17	  type StorageLocalRecord,
18	  type StorageRecordQuery,
19	  type StorageValidationIssue,
20	  type StorageValidationResult,
21	} from '../core';
22	import type { LocalMaterialAdapter, LocalMaterialAdapterError } from '../adapters';
23	import {
24	  selectStorageCsvMaterial,
25	  selectStorageCsvMaterialSummaries,
26	  selectStorageHourSummaries,
27	  selectStorageLastIssue,
28	  selectStorageLegacyMaterial,

30	  selectStorageLocalRecords,

36	export interface StorageLocalReader {
37	  getSnapshot(): StorageStateSnapshot;
38	  listLocalRecords(query?: StorageRecordQuery): readonly StorageLocalRecord[];
39	  listHistoryHours(): readonly StorageHourSummary[];
40	  listCsvMaterials(): readonly StorageCsvMaterialSummary[];
41	  getCsvMaterial(id: string): StorageCsvMaterial | undefined;
42	  listLegacyMaterials(): readonly StorageLegacyMaterialSummary[];
43	  getLegacyMaterial(key: string): StorageLegacyJsonMaterial | undefined;
44	  getLastIssue(): ReturnType<typeof selectStorageLastIssue>;
45	}
46	
47	export interface StorageLocalOperationResult {
48	  readonly ok: boolean;
49	  readonly validation: StorageValidationResult;
50	  readonly snapshot: StorageStateSnapshot;
51	  readonly error?: LocalMaterialAdapterError;
52	}
53	
54	export interface StorageLocalCsvOperationResult extends StorageLocalOperationResult {
55	  readonly material?: StorageCsvMaterial;
56	}
57	
58	export interface StorageLocalService extends StorageLocalReader {
59	  loadLocalRecords(materialId: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
60	  appendLocalRecords(records: readonly StorageLocalRecord[]): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
61	  loadHistoryMaterials(materialIds: readonly string[]): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
62	  createCsvFromLocalRecords(input: Omit<CreateStorageCsvMaterialInput, 'records'> & {
63	    readonly query?: StorageRecordQuery;
64	  }): Promise<StorageLocalCsvOperationResult>;
65	  saveLegacyJsonMaterial(key: string, value: unknown, acceptedAt: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
66	  copySnapshot(snapshotId: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
67	}
68	
69	export interface CreateStorageLocalServiceOptions {
70	  readonly adapter: LocalMaterialAdapter;
71	  readonly state?: StorageStateContainer;
72	  readonly recordsMaterialId?: string;
73	}
74	
75	function hasError(issues: readonly StorageValidationIssue[]): boolean {
76	  return issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error');
77	}
78	
79	function adapterIssue(error: LocalMaterialAdapterError): StorageValidationResult {
80	  return createStorageValidationResult([
81	    createStorageIssue(`adapter.${error.kind}`, 'adapter', error.message),
82	  ]);
83	}
84	
85	function failedWithValidation(
86	  state: StorageStateContainer,
87	  validation: StorageValidationResult,
88	): StorageLocalOperationResult {
89	  const firstError = validation.issues.find((issue) => issue.severity === 'error');
90	  if (firstError) {
91	    return {
92	      ok: false,
93	      validation,
94	      snapshot: state.setLastIssue({ code: firstError.code, message: firstError.message }),
95	    };
96	  }
97	
98	  return {
99	    ok: false,
100	    validation,
101	    snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
102	  };
103	}
104	
105	function failedWithAdapter(
106	  state: StorageStateContainer,
107	  error: LocalMaterialAdapterError,
108	): StorageLocalOperationResult {
109	  return {
110	    ok: false,
111	    validation: adapterIssue(error),
112	    snapshot: state.setLastIssue({ code: `adapter.${error.kind}`, message: error.message }),
113	    error,
114	  };
115	}
116	
117	function okResult(
118	  state: StorageStateContainer,
119	  validation: StorageValidationResult = createValidStorageResult(),
120	): StorageLocalOperationResult {
121	  state.setLastIssue(undefined);
122	  return {
123	    ok: true,
124	    validation,
125	    snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
126	  };
127	}
128	
129	export function createStorageLocalReader(
130	  snapshotProvider: () => StorageStateSnapshot,
131	): StorageLocalReader {
132	  return {
133	    getSnapshot() {
134	      return snapshotProvider();
135	    },
136	
137	    listLocalRecords(query) {
138	      return selectStorageLocalRecords(snapshotProvider(), query);
139	    },
140	
141	    listHistoryHours() {
142	      return selectStorageHourSummaries(snapshotProvider());
143	    },
144	
145	    listCsvMaterials() {
146	      return selectStorageCsvMaterialSummaries(snapshotProvider());
147	    },
148	
149	    getCsvMaterial(id) {
150	      return selectStorageCsvMaterial(snapshotProvider(), id);
151	    },
152	
153	    listLegacyMaterials() {
154	      return selectStorageLegacyMaterialSummaries(snapshotProvider());
155	    },
156	
157	    getLegacyMaterial(key) {
158	      return selectStorageLegacyMaterial(snapshotProvider(), key);
159	    },
160	
161	    getLastIssue() {
162	      return selectStorageLastIssue(snapshotProvider());
163	    },
164	  };
165	}
166	
167	export function createStorageLocalService(
168	  options: CreateStorageLocalServiceOptions,
169	): StorageLocalService {
170	  const state = options.state ?? createStorageState();
171	  const recordsMaterialId = options.recordsMaterialId ?? 'local-records';
172	  const reader = createStorageLocalReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
173	
174	  return {
175	    ...reader,
176	
177	    async loadLocalRecords(materialId) {
178	      const readResult = await options.adapter.readMaterial('records', materialId);
179	      if (!readResult.ok) {
180	        return failedWithAdapter(state, readResult.error);
181	      }
182	
183	      const normalized = normalizeStorageLocalRecords(readResult.value, 'records');
184	      if (!normalized.ok) {
185	        return failedWithValidation(state, normalized.validation);
186	      }
187	
188	      state.replaceRecords(normalized.records);
189	      return okResult(state, normalized.validation);
190	    },
191	
192	    async appendLocalRecords(records) {
193	      const normalized = normalizeStorageLocalRecords(records, 'records');
194	      if (!normalized.ok) {
195	        return failedWithValidation(state, normalized.validation);
196	      }
197	
198	      const merged = mergeStorageLocalRecords([...state.getSnapshot().records, ...normalized.records]);
199	      const writeResult = await options.adapter.writeMaterial('records', recordsMaterialId, merged);
200	      if (!writeResult.ok) {
201	        return failedWithAdapter(state, writeResult.error);
202	      }
203	
204	      state.replaceRecords(merged);
205	      return okResult(state, normalized.validation);
206	    },
207	
208	    async loadHistoryMaterials(materialIds) {
209	      const materials: StorageHistoryMaterial[] = [];
210	      const issues: StorageValidationIssue[] = [];
211	
212	      for (const materialId of materialIds) {
213	        const readResult = await options.adapter.readMaterial('history', materialId);
214	        if (!readResult.ok) {
215	          return failedWithAdapter(state, readResult.error);
216	        }
217	
218	        const normalized = normalizeStorageHistoryMaterial(readResult.value, `history.${materialId}`);
219	        issues.push(...normalized.validation.issues);
220	        if (normalized.material) {
221	          materials.push(normalized.material);
222	        }
223	      }
224	
225	      const validation = createStorageValidationResult(issues);
226	      if (hasError(validation.issues)) {
227	        return failedWithValidation(state, validation);
228	      }
229	
230	      const mergedRecords = mergeStorageHistoryMaterials(materials);
231	      state.replaceHistoryMaterials(materials);
232	      state.replaceRecords(mergedRecords);
233	      return okResult(state, validation);
234	    },
235	
236	    async createCsvFromLocalRecords(input) {
237	      const records = queryStorageLocalRecords(state.getSnapshot().records, input.query);
238	      const csvResult = createStorageCsvMaterial({
239	        ...input,
240	        records,
241	      });
242	
243	      if (!csvResult.ok || !csvResult.material) {
244	        return failedWithValidation(state, csvResult.validation);
245	      }
246	
247	      const writeResult = await options.adapter.writeMaterial('csv', csvResult.material.id, csvResult.material);
248	      if (!writeResult.ok) {
249	        return failedWithAdapter(state, writeResult.error);
250	      }
251	
252	      state.upsertCsvMaterial(csvResult.material);
253	      return {
254	        ...okResult(state, csvResult.validation),
255	        material: csvResult.material,
256	      };
257	    },
258	
259	    async saveLegacyJsonMaterial(key, value, acceptedAt) {
260	      const classified = classifyStorageLegacyJsonMaterial(key, value, acceptedAt);
261	      if (!classified.ok || !classified.material) {
262	        return failedWithValidation(state, classified.validation);
263	      }
264	
265	      const writeResult = await options.adapter.writeMaterial('legacy', classified.material.key, value);
266	      if (!writeResult.ok) {
267	        return failedWithAdapter(state, writeResult.error);
268	      }
269	
270	      state.upsertLegacyMaterial(classified.material);
271	      return okResult(state, classified.validation);
272	    },
273	
274	    async copySnapshot(snapshotId) {
275	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
276	      const writeResult = await options.adapter.writeMaterial('snapshot', snapshotId, snapshot);
277	      if (!writeResult.ok) {
278	        return failedWithAdapter(state, writeResult.error);
279	      }
280	
281	      return okResult(state);
282	    },
283	  };
284	}
285	
```

2	import os from 'node:os';
3	import path from 'node:path';
4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
5	import { registerSerialHandlers, cleanupSerialHandlers } from './serial-handlers';
6	import { registerNetworkHandlers, cleanupNetworkHandlers } from './network-handlers';
7	import { registerFileHandlers, cleanupFileHandlers } from './file-handlers';
8	
9	const platform = process.platform || os.platform();
10	const currentDir = fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url));
11	
12	let mainWindow: BrowserWindow | undefined;
13	
14	function getPreloadPath() {
15	  const preloadFolder = process.env.QUASAR_ELECTRON_PRELOAD_FOLDER ?? 'preload';
16	  const preloadExtension = process.env.QUASAR_ELECTRON_PRELOAD_EXTENSION ?? '.cjs';
17	
18	  return path.resolve(currentDir, path.join(preloadFolder, `index${preloadExtension}`));
19	}

21	async function createWindow() {
22	  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
23	    width: 1200,
24	    height: 800,
25	    minWidth: 960,
26	    minHeight: 640,
27	    useContentSize: true,
28	    show: false,
29	    webPreferences: {
30	      nodeIntegration: false,
31	      contextIsolation: true,
32	      sandbox: false,
33	      preload: getPreloadPath(),
34	    },
35	  });
36	
37	  registerSerialHandlers(mainWindow);
38	  registerNetworkHandlers(mainWindow);
39	  registerFileHandlers();
40	
41	  const appUrl = process.env.APP_URL;
42	  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {
43	    mainWindow?.show();
44	  });
45	
46	  mainWindow.webContents.once('did-finish-load', () => {
47	    if (mainWindow?.isVisible() === false) {
48	      mainWindow.show();
49	    }
50	  });
51	
52	  if (process.env.DEV && appUrl) {
53	    await mainWindow.loadURL(appUrl);
54	  } else {
55	    await mainWindow.loadFile('index.html');
56	  }
57	
58	  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {
59	    cleanupSerialHandlers();
60	    cleanupNetworkHandlers();
61	    cleanupFileHandlers();
62	    mainWindow = undefined;
63	  });
64	}
65	
66	void app.whenReady().then(createWindow);
67	
68	app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
69	  if (platform !== 'darwin') {
70	    app.quit();
71	  }
72	});
73	
74	app.on('activate', () => {
75	  if (mainWindow === undefined) {
76	    void createWindow();
77	  }
78	});

1	import { ipcMain, dialog, BrowserWindow } from 'electron';
2	import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
3	import type { SaveDialogOptions, OpenDialogOptions } from '../../src/shared/platform-bridge';
4	
5	const IPC_READ_TEXT_FILE = 'file:read-text';
6	const IPC_WRITE_TEXT_FILE = 'file:write-text';
7	const IPC_SHOW_SAVE_DIALOG = 'file:show-save-dialog';
8	const IPC_SHOW_OPEN_DIALOG = 'file:show-open-dialog';
9	
10	async function handleReadTextFile(_e: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, filePath: string): Promise<string> {
11	  return fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf-8');
12	}
13	
14	async function handleWriteTextFile(_e: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, filePath: string, content: string): Promise<void> {
15	  await fs.writeFile(filePath, content, 'utf-8');
16	}
17	
18	async function handleShowSaveDialog(e: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, opts: SaveDialogOptions): Promise<string | null> {
19	  const win = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(e.sender);
20	  const result = await dialog.showSaveDialog(win!, {
21	    title: opts.title,
22	    defaultPath: opts.defaultPath,
23	    filters: opts.filters as Electron.FileDialogFilter[] | undefined,
24	  });
25	  return result.canceled ? null : result.filePath ?? null;
26	}
27	
28	async function handleShowOpenDialog(e: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, opts: OpenDialogOptions): Promise<string | null> {
29	  const win = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(e.sender);
30	  const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win!, {
31	    title: opts.title,
32	    defaultPath: opts.defaultPath,
33	    filters: opts.filters as Electron.FileDialogFilter[] | undefined,
34	    properties: opts.multiple ? ['multiSelections'] : undefined,
35	  });
36	  return result.canceled ? null : result.filePaths[0] ?? null;
37	}
38	
39	export function registerFileHandlers(): void {
40	  ipcMain.handle(IPC_READ_TEXT_FILE, handleReadTextFile);
41	  ipcMain.handle(IPC_WRITE_TEXT_FILE, handleWriteTextFile);
42	  ipcMain.handle(IPC_SHOW_SAVE_DIALOG, handleShowSaveDialog);
43	  ipcMain.handle(IPC_SHOW_OPEN_DIALOG, handleShowOpenDialog);
44	}
45	
46	export function cleanupFileHandlers(): void {
47	  ipcMain.removeHandler(IPC_READ_TEXT_FILE);
48	  ipcMain.removeHandler(IPC_WRITE_TEXT_FILE);
49	  ipcMain.removeHandler(IPC_SHOW_SAVE_DIALOG);
50	  ipcMain.removeHandler(IPC_SHOW_OPEN_DIALOG);
51	}

1	import { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } from 'electron';
2	import {
3	  createRewriteBridgeInfo,
4	  REWRITE_PLATFORM_BRIDGE_KEY,
5	  type TransportBridge,
6	  type TransportBridgeEvent,
7	  type TransportCommandResult,
8	  type SerialPortCandidate,
9	  type TransportConnectConfig,
10	  type FileBridge,
11	  type SaveDialogOptions,
12	  type OpenDialogOptions,
13	} from '../../src/shared/platform-bridge';
14	
15	const IPC_ENUMERATE = 'transport:enumerate-serial-ports';
16	const IPC_CONNECT = 'transport:serial-connect';
17	const IPC_DISCONNECT = 'transport:serial-disconnect';
18	const IPC_WRITE = 'transport:serial-write';
19	const IPC_CLEANUP = 'transport:cleanup';
20	const IPC_EVENT_CHANNEL = 'transport:event';
21	
22	const IPC_NETWORK_CONNECT = 'transport:network-connect';
23	const IPC_NETWORK_DISCONNECT = 'transport:network-disconnect';
24	const IPC_NETWORK_WRITE = 'transport:network-write';
25	const IPC_NETWORK_CLEANUP = 'transport:network-cleanup';
26	
27	const IPC_READ_TEXT_FILE = 'file:read-text';
28	const IPC_WRITE_TEXT_FILE = 'file:write-text';
29	const IPC_SHOW_SAVE_DIALOG = 'file:show-save-dialog';
30	const IPC_SHOW_OPEN_DIALOG = 'file:show-open-dialog';
31	
32	const eventBuffer: TransportBridgeEvent[] = [];
33	const eventCallbacks: ((event: TransportBridgeEvent) => void)[] = [];
34	const connectionTypes = new Map<string, 'serial' | 'network'>();
35	
36	ipcRenderer.on(IPC_EVENT_CHANNEL, (_e, event: TransportBridgeEvent) => {
37	  if (event.kind === 'connected' && event.target?.role === 'tcp-server-client') {
38	    connectionTypes.set(event.connectionId, 'network');
39	  }
40	  if (event.kind === 'disconnected') {
41	    connectionTypes.delete(event.connectionId);
42	  }
43	
44	  eventBuffer.push(event);
45	  for (const cb of eventCallbacks) {
46	    try {
47	      cb(event);
48	    } catch {
49	      // ignore callback errors
50	    }
51	  }
52	});
53	
54	const transportBridge: TransportBridge = {
55	  async enumerateSerialPorts(): Promise<readonly SerialPortCandidate[]> {
56	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_ENUMERATE);
57	  },
58	
59	  async connect(config: TransportConnectConfig): Promise<TransportCommandResult> {
60	    if (config.kind === 'serial') {
61	      const result = await ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CONNECT, config);
62	      if (result.ok) connectionTypes.set(config.id, 'serial');
63	      return result;
64	    }
65	    const result = await ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_NETWORK_CONNECT, config);
66	    if (result.ok) connectionTypes.set(config.id, 'network');
67	    return result;
68	  },
69	
70	  async disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<TransportCommandResult> {
71	    const type = connectionTypes.get(connectionId);
72	    connectionTypes.delete(connectionId);
73	    if (type === 'serial') {
74	      return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_DISCONNECT, connectionId);
75	    }
76	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_NETWORK_DISCONNECT, connectionId);
77	  },
78	
79	  async write(connectionId: string, bytes: readonly number[]): Promise<TransportCommandResult> {
80	    const type = connectionTypes.get(connectionId);
81	    if (type === 'serial') {
82	      return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_WRITE, connectionId, bytes);
83	    }
84	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_NETWORK_WRITE, connectionId, bytes);
85	  },
86	
87	  async cleanup(): Promise<TransportCommandResult> {
88	    connectionTypes.clear();
89	    const [serialResult, networkResult] = await Promise.all([
90	      ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CLEANUP),
91	      ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_NETWORK_CLEANUP),
92	    ]);
93	    connectionTypes.clear();
94	    return {
95	      ok: serialResult.ok && networkResult.ok,
96	      events: [...serialResult.events, ...networkResult.events],
97	    };
98	  },
99	
100	  drainEvents(): readonly TransportBridgeEvent[] {
101	    return eventBuffer.splice(0, eventBuffer.length);
102	  },
103	
104	  onEvent(callback: (event: TransportBridgeEvent) => void): () => void {
105	    eventCallbacks.push(callback);
106	    return () => {
107	      const idx = eventCallbacks.indexOf(callback);
108	      if (idx >= 0) eventCallbacks.splice(idx, 1);
109	    };
110	  },
111	};
112	
113	const fileBridge: FileBridge = {
114	  async readTextFile(filePath: string): Promise<string> {
115	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_READ_TEXT_FILE, filePath);
116	  },
117	  async writeTextFile(filePath: string, content: string): Promise<void> {
118	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_WRITE_TEXT_FILE, filePath, content);
119	  },
120	  async showSaveDialog(opts: SaveDialogOptions): Promise<string | null> {
121	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_SHOW_SAVE_DIALOG, opts);
122	  },
123	  async showOpenDialog(opts: OpenDialogOptions): Promise<string | null> {
124	    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_SHOW_OPEN_DIALOG, opts);
125	  },
126	};
127	
128	const rewriteBridge = Object.freeze({
129	  getBridgeInfo: () => createRewriteBridgeInfo('0.0.0'),
130	  transport: transportBridge,
131	  file: fileBridge,
132	});
133	
134	contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld(REWRITE_PLATFORM_BRIDGE_KEY, rewriteBridge);
135	
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the connection feature's data structure. Let me provide you with a detailed analysis:

## Connection Feature Data Analysis

### How Connection Configs are Stored in State

Connection configurations are stored in the `ConnectionStateSnapshot` which contains:

```typescript
interface ConnectionStateSnapshot {
  readonly schemaVersion: typeof CONNECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  readonly configs: readonly TransportConfig[];        // Array of connection configs
  readonly runtimeFacts: readonly ConnectionRuntimeFact[]; // Runtime state for each connection
  readonly events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];  // Event history (max 50 events)
  readonly lastError?: TransportErrorSnapshot;         // Most recent error
}
```

**Key State Operations:**
- **`getSnapshot()`** - Returns a cloned snapshot of current state
- **`upsertConfig(config)`** - Adds or updates a connection config in state
- **`applyEvent(event)`** - Applies transport events to update runtime state
- **`replaceSnapshot(snapshot)`** - Replaces entire state with new snapshot
- **`resetSnapshot(snapshot)`** - Resets to empty or provided snapshot

**Connection Config Types:**
```typescript
type TransportConfig = 
  | SerialTransportConfig      // { id, kind: 'serial', portPath, baudRate, label?, autoConnect? }
  | TcpClientTransportConfig   // { id, kind: 'tcp-client', host, port, label?, autoConnect? }
  | TcpServerTransportConfig   // { id, kind: 'tcp-server', host, port, label?, autoConnect? }
  | UdpTransportConfig         // { id, kind: 'udp', localHost, localPort, remoteHost?, remotePort?, label?, autoConnect? }
```

### What `createConnectionService()` Accepts

The service factory accepts these options:

```typescript
interface CreateConnectionServiceOptions {
  readonly adapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;  // Required: transport adapter implementation
  readonly state?: ConnectionStateContainer;     // Optional: custom state container
  readonly now?: () => string;                   // Optional: timestamp generator
  readonly setTimeout?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown;  // Optional: custom timeout
  readonly clearTimeout?: (id: unknown) => void; // Optional: custom timeout canceler
}
```

### Available Operations

**Reader Operations (Query State):**
- **`getSnapshot()`** - Get complete state snapshot
- **`listTransportConfigs()`** - List all connection configurations
- **`listConnectionFacts()`** - List runtime facts for all connections
- **`getConnectionFact(connectionId)`** - Get specific connection's runtime state
- **`listConnectionSummaries()`** - Get connection summaries (lightweight view)
- **`listTransportTargets(query?)`** - List transport targets with optional filtering
- **`getLastTransportError()`** - Get most recent error
- **`listTransportEvents()`** - List all transport events
- **`getReconnectStatus(connectionId)`** - Get reconnect status for a connection

**Writer Operations (Modify State):**
- **`connect(config)`** - Connect using a transport configuration
- **`disconnect(connectionId)`** - Disconnect a specific connection
- **`write(request)`** - Write data to a connection
- **`drainAdapterEvents()`** - Process pending adapter events (handles auto-reconnect)
- **`cleanup()`** - Cleanup all connections
- **`discoverResources()`** - Discover available connection resources

**Operation Outcome:**
All operations return `ConnectionOperationOutcome`:
```typescript
interface ConnectionOperationOutcome {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly validation: ConnectionValidationOutcome;
  readonly snapshot: ConnectionStateSnapshot;
  readonly events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];
  readonly error?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
}
```

### Data Persistence

**Saving Configurations:**
- Use `state.upsertConfig(config)` to add/update connection configurations
- Configs are stored in `snapshot.configs` array, sorted by ID
- Each config maintains its associated `ConnectionRuntimeFact` in `snapshot.runtimeFacts`

**Loading Configurations:**
- Use `listTransportConfigs()` to retrieve all stored configurations
- Use `getConnectionFact(connectionId)` to get specific connection state
- Initial state can be loaded via `createConnectionState({ snapshot: savedSnapshot })`

**State Persistence Pattern:**
```typescript
// Save state
const snapshot = connectionService.getSnapshot();
localStorage.setItem('connections', JSON.stringify(snapshot));

// Load state
const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('connections'));
const state = createConnectionState({ snapshot: saved });
const service = createConnectionService({ adapter, state });
```

### Runtime State Management

Each connection maintains a `ConnectionRuntimeFact` containing:
- **Lifecycle state**: idle, connecting, connected, disconnecting, disconnected, error
- **Target information**: label, route label, availability
- **Counters**: connect attempts, bytes transferred, errors, etc.
- **Activity tracking**: last activity timestamp, last error
- **Reconnect state**: attempt count, next scheduled attempt

The state is immutable - all operations return new snapshots rather than modifying existing state.
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the frame feature's service and state architecture. Let me provide you with a detailed analysis:

## Frame Feature Analysis

### 1. Frame State Structure (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/state/frame-state.ts`)

The frame state is managed through a **FrameStateContainer** interface with the following structure:

```typescript
interface FrameStateSnapshot {
  readonly frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
  readonly selectedFrameId?: string;
}
```

**State Fields:**
- **frames**: Immutable array of frame assets
- **selectedFrameId**: Optional ID of the currently selected frame

**State Operations:**
- `getSnapshot()`: Returns current state
- `replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId?)`: Complete replacement
- `upsertFrame(frame)`: Insert or update a single frame
- `removeFrame(frameId)`: Delete a frame (also deselects if it was selected)
- `selectFrame(frameId)`: Change the selected frame

### 2. Frame Asset Structure (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/core/types.ts`)

**FrameAsset** contains:
- **id**: string
- **name**: string  
- **direction**: 'send' | 'receive'
- **fields**: FrameFieldDefinition[]
- **description**?: string
- **frameType**?: string
- **protocol**?: string
- **isFavorite**?: boolean
- **options**?: FrameOptionsDefinition (autoChecksum, bigEndian, includeLengthField)
- **identifierRules**?: IdentifierRule[]
- **createdAt**?: string
- **updatedAt**?: string

**FrameFieldDefinition** contains:
- **id**: string
- **name**: string
- **dataType**: uint8, int8, uint16, int16, uint32, int32, uint64, int64, float, double, bytes
- **length**: number
- **description**?: string
- **validOption**?: FrameChecksumDefinition
- **defaultValue**?: string
- **inputType**: 'input' | 'select' | 'radio' | 'expression'
- **configurable**: boolean
- **options**: FrameOptionDefinition[]
- **dataParticipationType**: 'direct' | 'indirect'
- **expressionConfig**?: ExpressionDefinition
- **bigEndian**?: boolean
- **isASCII**?: boolean
- **factor**?: number

### 3. Service API (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/services/frame-asset-service.ts`)

**createFrameAssetService()** signature:
```typescript
function createFrameAssetService(
  state: FrameStateContainer = createFrameState()
): FrameAssetService
```

**Accepts:**
- Optional `FrameStateContainer` (defaults to new state via `createFrameState()`)

**Returns:**
- `FrameAssetService` which extends `FrameAssetReader` with write operations

**Public API - Read Operations (FrameAssetReader):**
- `getSnapshot()`: Get current state snapshot
- `listFrames(query?)`: List frame summaries with optional filtering
- `findFrames(query?)`: Find full frame assets with filtering
- `getFrame(frameId)`: Get specific frame by ID
- `getSelectedFrame()`: Get currently selected frame
- `listFrameReferences(query?)`: Get reference options for frames
- `listFieldReferences(query?)`: Get field references across frames

**Public API - Write Operations:**
- `replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId?)`: Replace all frames
- `loadLegacyFrameConfig(value, selectedFrameId?)`: Migrate and load legacy config
- `selectFrame(frameId)`: Change selected frame
- `upsertFrame(frame)`: Insert or update single frame
- `removeFrame(frameId)`: Delete a frame

**Query Options:**
```typescript
interface FrameAssetQuery {
  readonly query?: string;           // Text search
  readonly direction?: 'send' | 'receive';
  readonly favoriteOnly?: boolean;
}
```

### 4. Additional Utilities

**Serialization/Deserialization:**
- `serializeFrames(frames)`: Convert frames to JSON string
- `deserializeFrames(text)`: Parse JSON or legacy format

**Operation Results:**
All write operations return `FrameAssetOperationResult`:
```typescript
interface FrameAssetOperationResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly validation: ValidationResult;
  readonly snapshot: FrameStateSnapshot;
}
```

**Validation:**
- All operations include validation with detailed issues
- Validation includes error severity and structured error codes
- Legacy migration includes validation issues

### 5. Key Design Patterns

1. **Immutable State**: State is never mutated directly, always returns new snapshots
2. **Cloning**: All returned frames are cloned for safety
3. **Validation-First**: All write operations validate before applying changes
4. **Type Safety**: Extensive use of readonly types and discriminated unions
5. **Separation of Concerns**: State, selectors, and services are in separate modules
6. **Legacy Support**: Built-in migration path for legacy frame configurations

The architecture follows a clean, functional approach with strong typing and immutable state management, making it easy to reason about frame data operations and ensuring data integrity through validation.
```

1	import {
2	  cloneFrameAsset,
3	  cloneFrameAssets,
4	  type FrameAsset,
5	  type ReadonlyFrameAsset,
6	} from '../core';
7	
8	export interface FrameStateSnapshot {
9	  readonly frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
10	  readonly selectedFrameId?: string;
11	}
12	
13	export interface FrameStateInitialValue {
14	  readonly frames?: readonly FrameAsset[];
15	  readonly selectedFrameId?: string;
16	}
17	
18	export interface FrameStateContainer {
19	  getSnapshot(): FrameStateSnapshot;
20	  replaceFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], selectedFrameId?: string): FrameStateSnapshot;
21	  upsertFrame(frame: FrameAsset): FrameStateSnapshot;
22	  removeFrame(frameId: string): FrameStateSnapshot;
23	  selectFrame(frameId: string | undefined): FrameStateSnapshot;
24	}
25	
26	function hasFrame(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], frameId: string | undefined): boolean {
27	  return typeof frameId === 'string' && frames.some((frame) => frame.id === frameId);
28	}
29	
30	function createSnapshot(
31	  frames: readonly FrameAsset[],
32	  selectedFrameId: string | undefined,
33	): FrameStateSnapshot {
34	  return {
35	    frames: cloneFrameAssets(frames),
36	    ...(selectedFrameId ? { selectedFrameId } : {}),
37	  };
38	}
39	
40	export function createFrameState(initialValue: FrameStateInitialValue = {}): FrameStateContainer {
41	  let frames = cloneFrameAssets(initialValue.frames ?? []);
42	  let selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, initialValue.selectedFrameId)
43	    ? initialValue.selectedFrameId
44	    : undefined;
45	
46	  return {
47	    getSnapshot() {
48	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
49	    },
50	
51	    replaceFrames(nextFrames, nextSelectedFrameId = selectedFrameId) {
52	      frames = cloneFrameAssets(nextFrames);
53	      selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, nextSelectedFrameId) ? nextSelectedFrameId : undefined;
54	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
55	    },
56	
57	    upsertFrame(frame) {
58	      const nextFrame = cloneFrameAsset(frame);
59	      const index = frames.findIndex((item) => item.id === frame.id);
60	
61	      if (index === -1) {
62	        frames = [...frames, nextFrame];
63	      } else {
64	        frames = frames.map((item, itemIndex) => (itemIndex === index ? nextFrame : item));
65	      }
66	
67	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
68	    },
69	
70	    removeFrame(frameId) {
71	      frames = frames.filter((frame) => frame.id !== frameId);
72	      if (selectedFrameId === frameId) {
73	        selectedFrameId = undefined;
74	      }
75	
76	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
77	    },
78	
79	    selectFrame(frameId) {
80	      selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, frameId) ? frameId : undefined;
81	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
82	    },
83	  };
84	}
85	
```

1	---
2	doc_type: quality
3	type: rewrite-frontend-checklist
4	status: draft
5	date: 2026-05-11
6	summary: 前端 UI 自检 checklist，review/验收时逐项勾选。与 rewrite-frontend-conventions.md 对应。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend

12	---
13	
14	# Rewrite 前端 UI 自检 Checklist
15	
16	对应规范：`codestable/quality/rewrite-frontend-conventions.md`。
17	
18	review 或自检时按分组逐项检查。勾选 `- [x]` 表示通过。
19	
20	---
21	
22	## A. 样式检查
23	
24	- [ ] 无硬编码 hex/rgb/hsl 色值（style、class、SCSS 中搜索 `#`、`rgb(`、`hsl(`）
25	- [ ] 无硬编码 px/rem/em 间距（padding、margin、gap 应走 token 或 Quasar class）
26	- [ ] 无硬编码 z-index（走 `rw-z-*` token）
27	- [ ] transition 无 `all`，只列具体属性
28	- [ ] CSS 动画只用 `transform` / `opacity`，无 layout 属性动画
29	- [ ] `will-change` 只用于 `transform` / `opacity`，无 `will-change: width/height`
30	- [ ] 弹窗宽度用语义 class（`rw-dialog-*`），无硬编码 `width: Npx`
31	- [ ] 无 inline style 直接消费 token（文本色、边框、背景 → 语义 class `rw-text-*`/`rw-divider-*`/`rw-panel-*`）
32	
33	## B. Quasar 组件检查
34	
35	- [ ] 无第三方 UI 组件库引入（ag-Grid、Lucide、vue-sonner 等）
36	- [ ] 颜色用 Quasar brand prop（`color="primary"`），无 CSS 覆盖组件视觉
37	- [ ] 禁用/dense/flat 等通过 prop 设置，无 CSS 模拟
38	- [ ] 删除操作有二次确认（`$q.dialog()` 或项目封装组件）
39	- [ ] 操作反馈用 `$q.notify()`，不用第三方 toast
40	- [ ] 图标用 Material Icons（`o_` 前缀），无第三方图标库
41	
42	## C. 表格检查
43	
44	- [ ] 数据表格用 QTable
45	- [ ] 行数可能 > 100 时启用了 `virtual-scroll` + `virtual-scroll-item-size` + 容器高度
46	- [ ] `row-key` 使用稳定唯一标识符（非 index）
47	- [ ] 列数 ≥ 3 时列定义抽到 `columns.ts`
48	- [ ] 空状态有 `#no-data` slot
49	- [ ] loading 用 QTable `:loading` prop
50	- [ ] 大数据集排序/分页走 service 层，非纯前端
51	
52	## D. 表单检查
53	
54	- [ ] 表单用 QForm + Quasar 表单组件，无原生 HTML 表单元素混用
55	- [ ] 校验用 QInput `:rules`（简单）或 feature service（复杂），无第三方校验库
56	- [ ] 校验错误走 `:error` + `:error-message`，不用 toast/q-tooltip 显示校验错误
57	- [ ] 禁用态用 `disable` / `readonly` prop，不用 CSS opacity/pointer-events
58	- [ ] 提交按钮有 `:loading` 或 `:disable` 防重复
59	- [ ] 超过 20 字段的表单已拆分为子组件
60	
61	## E. 弹窗检查
62	
63	- [ ] QDialog 使用 `v-model` 双向绑定（非单向 `:model-value`）
64	- [ ] 弹窗内有 `@hide` 清理定时器/订阅/表单
65	- [ ] 不需保持状态的弹窗用 `v-if`（非 `v-show`）
66	
67	## F. 性能检查
68	
69	- [ ] 高频数据路径（串口/网络/SCOE）有缓冲层，非逐条触发 Vue 响应式
70	- [ ] 大数组（>50元素）用 `shallowRef`，更新走整组替换
71	- [ ] 无 `watch(x, fn, { deep: true })` 监听大对象
72	- [ ] computed 返回对象/数组时引用稳定（无每次返回新引用的 .filter/.map）
73	- [ ] UI 定时器用 `requestAnimationFrame`（非 `setInterval`），且 onUnmounted 清理
74	- [ ] v-for 的 `:key` 是稳定唯一 ID（非 index）
75	- [ ] 模板中无内联 `.filter()` / `.map()` / `.reduce()`
76	- [ ] 仪表盘/总览页多区域布局用了 `v-memo` 限制重渲染
77	
78	## G. 数据展示检查
79	
80	- [ ] 数字有格式化（千分位、有效精度），非原始值直出
81	- [ ] 时间格式统一（`YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss`），非 ISO 字符串或时间戳
82	- [ ] 状态用 QBadge + 状态色映射（positive/warning/negative/info），非裸文字+颜色
83	- [ ] 空值显示 `--`，非 `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`
84	- [ ] 物理量有单位标注（`12.5 V`、`25.3 ℃`）
85	- [ ] 长文本有截断 + QTooltip
86	
87	## H. Electron 检查
88	
89	- [ ] 高频 IPC 已批量化（main 端缓冲，16ms 批量发送）
90	- [ ] 单窗口 DOM 节点数 ≤ 3000
91	- [ ] 折叠面板/隐藏区域用 `v-if`（非 `v-show`）
92	
93	## I. Composable 检查
94	
95	- [ ] 异步操作用 `useAsyncAction()`，无手写 `operatingIds` Set + try-finally 清理
96	- [ ] 页面轮询用 `usePolling()`，无手写 rAF + disposed + 节流逻辑
97	- [ ] 通知用 `useNotify()`，无直接 `$q.notify({ type: 'positive/negative', ... })` 散落
98	- [ ] StatusBadge 使用泛型 `statusMap` 配置，无硬编码业务状态枚举
99	- [ ] 页面状态按 O1 分组声明（Service → 业务数据 → 查询/筛选 → UI → 派生 → 操作）
100	- [ ] 所有 `.catch()` / `catch {}` 非空，至少 `console.error`/`console.warn`（O2）
101	- [ ] 2+ 文件中的同一代码模式（标签映射、stable key、操作样板、默认值工厂）已提取（O3）
102	- [ ] 选项数组和标签映射在模块级定义，非 setup 内（O4）
103	
```

1	---
2	doc_type: quality
3	type: rewrite-frontend-conventions
4	status: draft
5	date: 2026-05-11
6	summary: 前端 UI 规范，讨论/设计/实施时参照。每条规则一句话 + 简短原因 + 一个示例。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend
10	  - conventions
11	  - quasar
12	  - performance
13	---
14	
15	# Rewrite 前端 UI 规范
16	
17	样式 token 规则和 UnoCSS 结构性布局职责划分见 CLAUDE.md "目录与职责"，token 定义见 `rewrite/src/css/tokens/`，本文不重复。代码质量规则见 `rewrite-quality-rules.md`。自检 checklist 见 `rewrite-frontend-checklist.md`。
18	
19	---
20	
21	## 1. Quasar 组件使用
22	
23	### Q1. UI 需求优先从 Quasar 内建方案解决
24	
25	不引入第三方 UI 库（ag-Grid、Lucide、vue-sonner 等）。
26	
27	```typescript
28	// ❌ 第三方 toast
29	import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'; toast.success('ok');
30	// ❌ 第三方图标
31	import { Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';
32	
33	// ✅ Quasar 内建
34	$q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: 'ok' });
35	<q-icon name="o_delete" />
36	```
37	
38	### Q2. Quasar prop 控制视觉行为，不用 CSS 覆盖
39	
40	```vue
41	<!-- ❌ -->
42	<q-input style="padding: 4px; height: 32px;" />
43	<q-btn style="box-shadow: none; background: transparent;" />
44	
45	<!-- ✅ -->
46	<q-input dense />
47	<q-btn flat color="primary" />
48	```
49	
50	### Q3. 颜色使用 Quasar brand prop，不硬编码
51	
52	```vue
53	<!-- ❌ -->
54	<q-btn style="background: #1f6feb; color: white;" />
55	<q-badge style="color: green;">已连接</q-badge>
56	
57	<!-- ✅ -->
58	<q-btn color="primary" />
59	<q-badge color="positive">已连接</q-badge>
60	```
61	
62	### Q4. 确认和反馈走 Quasar plugin
63	
64	删除确认用 `$q.dialog()`，操作反馈用 `$q.notify()`。不用 `window.confirm/alert`。
65	
66	```typescript
67	// ❌
68	if (window.confirm('确定删除？')) doDelete();
69	
70	// ✅
71	$q.dialog({ title: '确认删除', message: '不可恢复', cancel: true }).onOk(doDelete);
72	```
73	
74	### Q5. 删除操作必须二次确认
75	
76	级联删除用项目封装的二次确认组件（需输入关键词）。批量操作必须显示进度。
77	
78	---
79	
80	## 2. 表格
81	
82	### T1. 表格用 QTable，大列表开虚拟滚动
83	
84	行数可能超 100 必须开 `virtual-scroll` + 设 `virtual-scroll-item-size` + 设容器高度。
85	
86	```vue
87	<!-- ❌ 10000 行全渲染 -->
88	<q-table :rows="allData" />
89	
90	<!-- ✅ -->
91	<q-table
92	  :rows="data" virtual-scroll
93	  :virtual-scroll-item-size="48"
94	  :table-style="{ maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)' }"
95	  :rows-per-page-options="[0]"
96	  row-key="id"
97	/>
98	```
99	
100	### T2. 列数 ≥ 3 或有自定义渲染时，列定义抽到 `columns.ts`
101	
102	列宽策略：文本列自适应、状态列设 min-width、操作列固定宽度。不用百分比宽度。QTable `headerStyle` / `style` 中使用 px 值是 API 要求，不视为硬编码违规。
103	
104	### T3. 大数据集排序和分页走 service 层
105	
106	数据量 > 100 行时排序由 feature service 处理，分页用 QTable server-side 模式 + `@request`。不前端排序分页混合。
107	
108	### T4. 空状态和 loading 必须显式处理
109	
110	```vue
111	<q-table :loading="isLoading">
112	  <template #no-data>
113	    <div class="text-center q-pa-lg text-muted">暂无数据</div>
114	  </template>
115	</q-table>
116	```
117	
118	### T5. 行选中用 QTable selection，选中色走 token
119	
120	```vue
121	<!-- ❌ 在数据对象上加 _selected 字段，硬编码背景色 -->
122	:style="{ background: selectedRows.includes(row) ? '#e8f1ff' : '' }"
123	
124	<!-- ✅ -->
125	<q-table selection="multiple" v-model:selected="selected" />
126	```
127	
128	---
129	
130	## 3. 表单
131	
132	### F1. 表单用 QForm + Quasar 表单组件
133	
134	不混用 HTML 原生表单元素。校验用 QInput `:rules` prop（简单）或 feature service（复杂）。不用第三方校验库。
135	
136	```typescript
137	// ✅ 简单校验
138	<q-input v-model="name" :rules="[val => !!val || '请输入名称']" />
139	
140	// ✅ 复杂校验在 service 层
141	const result = await taskService.validateConfig(config);
142	```
143	
144	### F2. 校验错误用组件 error slot，不用 toast
145	
146	错误信息走 `:error` + `:error-message`，颜色走 `status-danger` token。
147	
148	### F3. 超过 20 个字段必须拆分子组件
149	
150	每个子组件管理自己的响应式数据，隔离 Vue 依赖追踪范围。动态字段超 50 用 QVirtualScroll 承载。QInput 校验用 `lazy-rules="ondemand"`。
151	
152	### F4. 提交按钮必须 disable/loading 防重复
153	
154	```vue
155	<q-btn label="提交" :loading="isSubmitting" @click="submit" />
156	```
157	
158	---
159	
160	## 4. 弹窗
161	
162	### D1. 弹窗用 QDialog，双向绑定 + @hide 清理
163	
164	```vue
165	<!-- ❌ 单向绑定，无法关闭 -->
166	<q-dialog :model-value="show">
167	
168	<!-- ✅ -->
169	<q-dialog v-model="show" @hide="resetForm">
170	  <q-card>...</q-card>
171	</q-dialog>
172	```
173	
174	### D2. 弹窗宽度用语义 class，不硬编码
175	
176	定义在 `css/tokens/_size.scss` + `css/layers/_utilities.scss`：
177	
178	| class | min-width | 用途 |
179	|-------|-----------|------|
180	| `rw-dialog-sm` | 400px | 单字段确认、简单选择 |
181	| `rw-dialog-md` | 560px | 表单、配置编辑 |
182	| `rw-dialog-lg` | 720px | 多字段编辑、预览 |
183	| `rw-dialog-xl` | 960px | 复合编辑器（任务编辑器、帧编辑器） |
184	
185	所有 class 带 `max-width: 80vw` 响应式下限。禁止在 `<q-card>` 或 scoped SCSS 中硬编码 `min-width`/`max-width` px 值。
186	
187	```vue
188	<!-- ❌ -->
189	<q-card style="width: 600px; max-width: 80vw;">
190	
191	<!-- ✅ -->
192	<q-card class="rw-dialog-md">
193	```
194	
195	---
196	
197	## 5. 页面布局
198	
199	### L1. 页面内边距用 `p-page` / `p-page-compact` UnoCSS class
200	
201	工具栏与内容区间距用 `gap-4`。结构性布局（间距、flex、grid、display、position、sizing）全部走 UnoCSS utility class，不在 `<style>` 中声明。
202	
203	### L2. 侧边栏用 QDrawer，宽度走 `app-drawer` token（232px）
204	
205	### L3. 桌面不做移动端适配
206	
207	不用 xs/sm 断点、不引入 touch 手势库。响应式仅处理窗口缩小，下限 `breakpoint.page-compact`（720px）。
208	
209	---
210	
211	## 6. 颜色与状态色
212	
213	### C1. 颜色只用 semantic-colors token 或 Quasar brand 色
214	
215	禁止在 style/class/SCSS 中硬编码 hex/rgb/hsl。
216	
217	```scss
218	// ✅ SCSS
219	.error-text { color: rw-color('status-danger'); }
220	// ✅ CSS 变量
221	background: var(--rw-color-surface-selected);
222	// ✅ Quasar
223	<q-badge color="negative">
224	```
225	
226	### C2. 状态色固定映射
227	
228	| 语义 | Token | Quasar color |
229	|------|-------|-------------|
230	| 成功/正常/在线 | `status-success` | `positive` |
231	| 警告/注意 | `status-warning` | `warning` |
232	| 错误/失败/离线 | `status-danger` | `negative` |
233	| 信息/提示 | `status-info` | `info` |
234	| 选中/激活 | `surface-selected` | — |
235	| 禁用/静默 | `text-muted` | — |
236	
237	### C3. 文字颜色分级
238	
239	| 层级 | Token | 用途 |
240	|------|-------|------|
241	| 主要 | `text-primary` | 标题、重要数据 |
242	| 默认 | `text-default` | 正文、表格内容 |
243	| 次要 | `text-secondary` | 描述、辅助信息 |
244	| 弱化 | `text-muted` | 时间戳、placeholder |
245	| 最弱 | `text-subtle` | 禁用态、hint |
246	
247	### C4. Token 消费必须走语义 class，禁止 inline style 消费 token
248	
249	禁止在模板 inline style 中直接使用 color/border/background token。已有语义 class 的 token 必须用 class；同一 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次但尚无 class 时，先提取语义 class 再使用。
250	
251	```vue
252	<!-- ❌ -->
253	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)">ID</span>
254	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)">{{ value }}</span>
255	<div style="border-bottom: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)">
256	
257	<!-- ✅ -->
258	<span class="rw-text-label">ID</span>
259	<span class="rw-text-value">{{ value }}</span>
260	<div class="rw-divider-b">
261	```
262	
263	以下语义 class 在 `css/layers/_utilities.scss` 中统一定义：
264	
265	**文本色**
266	
267	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
268	|-------|------|------|
269	| `rw-text-label` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)` | 字段标签、小标题 |
270	| `rw-text-value` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)` | 字段值、重要数据 |
271	| `rw-text-desc` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-secondary)` | 描述、辅助文字 |
272	| `rw-text-error` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-danger)` | 错误提示 |
273	| `rw-text-warn` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-warning)` | 警告提示 |
274	
275	**边框**
276	
277	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
278	|-------|------|------|
279	| `rw-divider-b` | `border-bottom: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
280	| `rw-divider-t` | `border-top: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
281	| `rw-divider-l` | `border-left: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 侧边面板分隔 |
282	
283	**面板 / 弹窗**
284	
285	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
286	|-------|------|------|
287	| `rw-panel-base` | `background: var(--rw-color-surface-base)` | 基础面板背景 |
288	| `rw-dialog-sm/md/lg/xl` | 弹窗宽度 | 见 §4 D2 |
289	
290	不在上表中的 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次时，按同一规则提取并补充。
291	
292	---
293	
294	## 7. 数据展示
295	
296	### V1. 数字格式化
297	
298	整数不带小数，大数加千分位，百分比带 `%`，浮点保留有效精度。格式化函数放 `shared/`。
299	
300	```vue
301	<!-- ❌ --> <span>{{ rawValue }}</span>  <!-- 1234567.890000001 -->
302	<!-- ✅ --> <span>{{ formatNumber(rawValue) }}</span>  <!-- 1,234,567.89 -->
303	```
304	
305	### V2. 时间格式化
306	
307	日期时间 `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss`，仅日期 `YYYY-MM-DD`。相对时间只用于实时日志，不用于历史记录。不直接展示 ISO 字符串或时间戳。
308	
309	### V3. 状态用 QBadge + 状态色，空值显示 `--`
310	
311	不显示 `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`。不裸用文字+颜色替代 badge。
312	
313	状态 badge 组件必须泛型化，不硬编码特定业务状态枚举。通过 `statusMap` 配置映射：
314	
315	```vue
316	<!-- ❌ 硬编码 ConnectionLifecycleStatus -->
317	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" />
318	
319	<!-- ✅ 泛型 + 外部映射 -->
320	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" :status-map="connectionStatusMap" />
321	<StatusBadge :status="task.state" :status-map="taskStatusMap" />
322	```
323	
324	`statusMap` 类型：`Record<string, { label: string; color: string }>`。各 feature 在自己的 `components/` 或 `types` 中定义对应的映射常量。
325	
326	### V4. 物理量必须标注单位，长文本截断 + QTooltip
327	
328	电压 `12.5 V`，温度 `25.3 ℃`，百分比 `85.3%`（无空格）。超长文本用 CSS 截断，QTooltip 展示全文。
329	
330	---
331	
332	## 8. 性能
333	
334	> 本章规则全部来源于旧系统实际踩坑，是 rewrite 性能底线。
335	
336	### P1. 高频数据必须缓冲后批量刷新
337	
338	串口/网络/SCOE 数据不得逐条触发 Vue 响应式。用 rAF（16ms）或定时器缓冲，每帧批量刷一次。UI 刷新频率与数据源频率解耦。
339	
340	```typescript
341	// ❌ 旧系统：每包数据直接穿透到 store
342	serialPort.on('data', data => store.handleReceivedData(data));
343	
344	// ✅ 缓冲层
345	let pending: Frame[] = [];
346	let rafId: number | null = null;
347	function pushFrame(frame: Frame) {
348	  pending.push(frame);
349	  if (!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
350	}
351	function flush() {
352	  displayData.value = [...pending]; pending = [];
353	  rafId = null;
354	}
355	```
356	
357	### P2. 大数组/大对象用 shallowRef，整组替换
358	
359	超 50 元素的数组、嵌套超 2 层的对象用 `shallowRef`。更新时 `arr.value = newArr`，不用 `.push()` / `.splice()`。第三方实例和历史数据用 `markRaw()`。
360	
361	```typescript
362	// ❌
363	const list = reactive<Frame[]>([]); list.push(newFrame);
364	// ✅
365	const list = shallowRef<Frame[]>([]); list.value = [...list.value, newFrame];
366	```
367	
368	### P3. 禁止 deep watch 大对象
369	
370	超 20 属性的对象或超 10 元素的数组禁止 `watch(x, fn, { deep: true })`。改为监听具体 computed、在变更点主动调用。
371	
372	```typescript
373	// ❌
374	watch(() => config, handler, { deep: true });
375	// ✅
376	watch(() => config.groups.length, () => rebuild());
377	// 或在变更点显式调用 rebuild()
378	```
379	
380	### P4. computed 返回对象/数组必须保持引用稳定
381	
382	避免 `.filter()` / `.map()` 直接返回（每次新引用）。Vue 3.4+ 用 computed 的 oldValue 参数比较。
383	
384	推荐模式：表格行数据、筛选结果等派生数组用 `shallowRef` + `watch` 手动更新，不用 `computed` + `.map()`/`.filter()`。
385	
386	```typescript
387	// ❌ 每次 frameList 变化都创建全新数组，触发下游全量 diff
388	const tableRows = computed(() =>
389	  frameList.value.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 })),
390	);
391	
392	// ✅ shallowRef + watch，整组替换（与 P2 一致）
393	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
394	watch(frameList, (list) => {
395	  tableRows.value = list.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 }));
396	}, { immediate: true });
397	```
398	
399	少量数据（< 50 条）时 computed 也勉强可用，但后续页面数据量可能增大，统一使用 shallowRef 模式减少认知负担。
400	
401	### P5. 定时器用 requestAnimationFrame，不用 setInterval
402	
403	UI 更新和动画驱动用 rAF。长周期轮询（≥1s）可用 setInterval，但必须 onUnmounted 清理。
404	
405	```typescript
406	// ❌ 旧系统：setInterval(fn, 500) 更新表格
407	// ✅ rAF + 节流
408	let last = 0;
409	function loop(now: number) {
410	  if (now - last >= 500) { updateTable(); last = now; }
411	  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
412	}
413	```
414	
415	### P6. v-for 必须用稳定唯一 key，不用 index
416	
417	```vue
418	<!-- ❌ --> <div v-for="(item, i) in list" :key="i">
419	<!-- ✅ --> <div v-for="item in list" :key="item.id">
420	```
421	
422	### P7. 仪表盘用 v-memo 限制重渲染范围
423	
424	```vue
425	<ConnectionPanel v-memo="[connectionState]" :state="connectionState" />
426	<FrameMetrics v-memo="[frameSnapshot]" :data="frameSnapshot" />
427	```
428	
429	### P8. 模板中禁止内联 .filter() / .map() / .reduce()
430	
431	提取到 computed。
432	
433	---
434	
435	## 9. CSS 动画
436	
437	### A1. transition 禁用 `all`，只列具体属性
438	
439	```css
440	/* ❌ */ transition: all 0.3s;
441	/* ✅ */ transition: transform 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease;
442	```
443	
444	### A2. 动画只用 transform 和 opacity（composite 属性）
445	
446	禁止动画 width/height/top/left/margin 等 layout 属性。状态指示器动画用 CSS @keyframes，不用 JS setInterval 驱动。will-change 只用于 transform/opacity，优先用 `transform: translateZ(0)` 替代。
447	
448	---
449	
450	## 10. Electron UI
451	
452	### E1. IPC 高频数据必须批量化
453	
454	main 进程缓冲串口/网络数据，16ms 批量发送一次。renderer 侧走 P1 缓冲模式。低频操作可直接 IPC。
455	
456	### E2. 提供硬件加速开关
457	
458	应用设置中提供开关，启动时处理 `gpu-process-crashed` 事件。支持 `--disable-gpu` 参数。
459	
460	### E3. 控制单窗口 DOM 节点 ≤ 3000
461	
462	虚拟滚动、懒渲染、v-if（非 v-show）是主要手段。折叠面板折叠时不渲染内容。
463	
464	---
465	
466	## 11. 页面 Composable 模式
467	
468	页面中以下三种模式不得手写，必须使用对应 composable。
469	
470	### CM1. 异步操作用 `useAsyncAction()`
471	
472	操作锁（operatingIds）、try-finally 清理、错误通知统一由 composable 管理。
473	
474	```typescript
475	// ❌ 手写 operatingIds Set + try-finally
476	const operatingIds = ref<Set<string>>(new Set());
477	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
478	  operatingIds.value = new Set(operatingIds.value).add(s.connectionId);
479	  try { /* ... */ }
480	  finally { const next = new Set(operatingIds.value); next.delete(s.connectionId); operatingIds.value = next; }
481	}
482	
483	// ✅ composable
484	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
485	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
486	  await execute(s.connectionId, async () => {
487	    const result = await service.connect(config);
488	    // composable 负责 finally 清理 + 错误时 $q.notify
489	  });
490	}
491	```
492	
493	composable 返回：
494	- `isOperating(id: string): boolean` — 查询操作锁
495	- `execute<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined>` — 带锁执行，自动清理，失败时 `$q.notify({ type: 'negative' })`
496	
497	### CM2. 页面轮询用 `usePolling()`
498	
499	rAF + 节流 + disposed 检查 + onUnmounted 清理统一由 composable 管理。
500	
501	```typescript
502	// ❌ 手写 rAF + disposed + 节流
503	let rafId = 0; let lastPollTime = 0; const disposed = ref(false);
504	function tick(now: number) { /* ... */ rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }
505	onMounted(() => { rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); });
506	onBeforeUnmount(() => { disposed.value = true; if (rafId) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); });
507	
508	// ✅ composable
509	const { start } = usePolling(() => refreshSummaries(), 500);
510	onMounted(start);
511	```
512	
513	composable 返回：
514	- `start(): void` — 启动轮询
515	- `stop(): void` — 停止轮询
516	- `restart(): void` — 重启轮询
517	- 内部自动 onUnmounted 清理
518	
519	### CM3. 通知用 `useNotify()`
520	
521	封装 `$q.notify` 的成功/失败/警告模式，统一消息格式。
522	
523	```typescript
524	const notify = useNotify();
525	notify.success('连接已创建');
526	notify.error('连接失败', result.error?.message);
527	notify.info('操作已取消');
528	```
529	
530	---
531	
532	## 12. 页面代码组织
533	
534	### O1. 状态声明按用途分组
535	
536	页面 `<script setup>` 中的 ref/computed/shallowRef 按以下顺序声明，组间空一行：
537	
538	```typescript
539	// ===== Service 引用 =====
540	const service = runtime.features.xxxService;
541	
542	// ===== 业务数据 =====
543	const items = ref<readonly Xxx[]>([]);
544	
545	// ===== 查询/筛选 =====
546	const searchText = ref('');
547	const statusFilter = ref<Status | ''>('');
548	
549	// ===== UI 状态 =====
550	const showDialog = ref(false);
551	const selectedIds = ref<string[]>([]);
552	
553	// ===== 派生数据 =====
554	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
555	
556	// ===== 操作 =====
557	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
558	const notify = useNotify();
559	```
560	
561	### O2. 禁止静默吞错误
562	
563	所有 catch 块必须至少 log（`console.error`/`console.warn`）或 notify。空 catch 体（`catch {}` / `catch () {}`）禁止出现。runtime tick、service 调用、文件 I/O、computed 内部、异步操作一律适用。
564	
565	```typescript
566	// ❌ 空 catch 静默吞错误
567	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch(() => {});
568	try { buildPreview(); } catch { /* nothing */ }
569	
570	// ✅ 至少记录错误
571	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
572	try { buildPreview(); } catch (err) { console.error('preview failed', err); }
573	```
574	
575	### O3. 2+ 处出现的同一代码模式必须提取
576	
577	标签映射、选项数组、stable key 管理、数组操作样板、默认值工厂等，出现在 2+ 文件/函数中时，提取到 composable、shared 常量或辅助函数。不允许复制粘贴后微调。
578	
579	### O4. 不变数据定义在模块级
580	
581	选项数组、标签映射、枚举→标签 Record 等不随组件实例变化的数据，定义在 `<script setup>` 外（模块级常量）。setup 每次挂载都会执行，不应在其中重建不变数据。
582	
```

6	summary: 前端速查卡——写新页面/新组件前花 30 秒扫一遍。含 shared/ 完整 API 索引 + 规范红线 + 新页面检查清单。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend

11	---
12	
13	# Rewrite 前端速查卡
14	
15	> 写新页面/新组件前先看。详细规则见 `rewrite-frontend-conventions.md`。
16	> shared/ 所有 API 从 `@/shared` 或 `@/shared/composables` 导入。shared 不 import 任何 feature。
17	
18	## composables — 页面必用
19	
20	| composable | 用途 | 替代什么 |
21	|-----------|------|----------|
22	| `useAsyncAction()` | 异步操作防重 + 自动错误通知 | 手写 operatingIds + try/finally |
23	| `usePolling(fn, ms)` | rAF 轮询 + 自动清理 | 手写 rAF / disposed / 节流 |
24	| `useNotify()` | 统一消息通知 | 散落 `$q.notify()` |
25	| `useStableKeys(prefix?)` | v-for 稳定 key 生成 | index key |
26	
27	```typescript
28	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction()          // execute: 加锁→执行→解锁→失败 notify
29	const { start, stop } = usePolling(() => refresh(), 1000)  // 自动 onUnmounted 清理
30	const notify = useNotify()                                  // success / error / info / warning
31	const { keys, syncKeys } = useStableKeys('step')            // keys.value[i] 始终稳定
32	```
33	
34	## utils/ — 纯工具函数
35	
36	写新代码前先查，避免重复造轮子。
37	
38	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
39	|------|------|------|
40	| `deepClone` | `<T>(value: T) → T` | structuredClone 封装 |
41	| `cloneUnknownValue` | `(value: unknown) → unknown` | 递归拷贝（不依赖 structuredClone） |
42	| `formatElapsed` | `(ms: number) → string` | 毫秒 → "Xms" / "Xs" / "Xm Ys" / "Xh Ym" |
43	| `defaultNow` | `() → string` | ISO 8601 时间戳 |
44	
45	## expression/ — 表达式编译与求值
46	
47	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
48	|------|------|------|
49	| `compileExpression` | `(text, functions?) → CompileResult` | 编译单条表达式 |
50	| `compileConditional` | `(branches, fallback?, functions?) → ConditionalCompileResult` | 编译条件分支 |
51	| `compileGroup` | `(expressions, functions?) → GroupCompileResult` | 编译表达式组（含依赖排序） |
52	| `evaluate` | `(compiled, variables) → EvalResult` | 求值单条表达式 |
53	| `evaluateConditional` | `(compiled, variables) → ConditionalEvalResult` | 求值条件分支 |
54	| `evaluateGroup` | `(group, variables) → GroupEvalResult` | 求值表达式组 |
55	| `defaultMathFunctions` | `FunctionTable` | 内置数学函数（abs/floor/ceil/round/min/max/sqrt/pow/sin/cos/tan/log/exp） |
56	
57	**依赖分析**: `extractDependencies(ast) → Set<string>`, `kahnSort(expressions) → { order } | { cycle }`
58	
59	**核心类型**: `VariableMap = ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>`, `VariableValue = number | string | boolean`, `FunctionTable = ReadonlyMap<string, (...args: number[]) => number>`
60	
61	## condition-operators/ — 条件匹配
62	
63	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
64	|------|------|------|
65	| `compareValues` | `(actual, threshold, operator) → boolean` | 通用值比较 |
66	
67	**运算符**: `eq` `neq` `gt` `lt` `gte` `lte` `contains` `change` `any`
68	
69	**类型**: `ComparisonOperator` — 上述运算符的联合类型
70	
71	## timer/ — 定时器管理
72	
73	| 类 | 用途 |
74	|----|------|
75	| `TimerRegistry` | 分组定时器注册表，全局暂停/恢复 |
76	
77	```typescript
78	const timers = new TimerRegistry()
79	timers.register('tick', () => poll(), 1000, 'polling')
80	timers.pauseAll()
81	timers.resumeAll()
82	timers.clearGroup('polling')
83	```
84	
85	## types/ — 共享类型
86	
87	| 类型 | 用途 |
88	|------|------|
89	| `ReadonlyDeep<T>` | 递归将 T 及嵌套属性设为 readonly |
90	| `ValidationIssue` | `{ severity: 'error' | 'warning', code, path, message }` |
91	| `ValidationResult` | `{ valid, issues: readonly ValidationIssue[] }` |
92	
93	## platform-bridge.ts — 平台能力接口
94	
95	定义 renderer 与桌面平台通信的 typed 接口。**不直接消费**，通过 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 间接使用。
96	
97	- `TransportBridge` — 串口/TCP/UDP 连接、写入、事件
98	- `FileBridge` — 文件读写、对话框
99	- `RewritePlatformBridge` — 组合接口
100	
101	---
102	
103	## 语义 class（不用 inline style 消费 token）
104	
105	```
106	文本：rw-text-label / rw-text-value / rw-text-desc / rw-text-error / rw-text-warn
107	边框：rw-divider-b / rw-divider-t / rw-divider-l
108	面板：rw-panel-base
109	弹窗：rw-dialog-sm / rw-dialog-md / rw-dialog-lg / rw-dialog-xl
110	滚动：rw-dialog-scroll-body
111	```
112	
113	完整表见 conventions §6 C4。
114	
115	## 绝对禁止
116	
117	- `catch {}` / `catch () {}` 空错误吞没（O2）
118	- `style="color: var(--rw-color-...)"` inline token → 用语义 class（C4）
119	- `style="width: Npx"` 硬编码像素 → 用 UnoCSS class 或 rw-dialog-*（C4/D2）
120	- `v-for ... :key="i"` index key → 用稳定 ID（P6）
121	- 组件超 300 行不拆
122	- 选项数组/标签映射写在 setup 内 → 放模块级或常量文件（O4）
123	- 同一逻辑出现 2+ 处不提取（O3）
124	- `any` 类型（D6）
125	
126	## 状态声明顺序（O1）
127	
128	```
129	Service 引用 → 业务数据 → 查询/筛选 → UI 状态 → 派生数据 → 操作
130	```
131	
132	## 新页面检查清单
133	
134	1. composable 用了没？（useAsyncAction / usePolling / useNotify）
135	2. shared/ 已有工具函数查了没？（deepClone / formatElapsed / compareValues）
136	3. 标签/选项提取了没？（模块级常量文件，O3/O4）
137	4. 语义 class 用了没？（grep `style=".*var(--rw-` 和 `style="width: \d`)
138	5. v-for key 稳定吗？（grep `:key="i"` / `:key="idx"`）
139	6. catch 都有处理吗？（grep `catch {}` / `catch () {}`）
140	7. 组件超 300 行吗？该拆吗？
141	8. shallowRef 用对了吗？（>50 元素数组必须用，P2）
142	
```

1	import {
2	  cloneConnectionStateSnapshot,
3	  createEmptyConnectionSnapshot,
4	  reduceTransportEvent,
5	  upsertConnectionConfig,
6	  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
7	  type NormalizedTransportEventInput,
8	  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
9	  type TransportConfig,
10	} from '../core';
11	
12	export interface ConnectionStateInitialValue {
13	  readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot;
14	}
15	
16	export interface ConnectionStateContainer {
17	  getSnapshot(): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
18	  replaceSnapshot(snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
19	  upsertConfig(config: TransportConfig): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
20	  applyEvent(event: NormalizedTransportEventInput): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
21	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
22	}
23	
24	export function createConnectionState(
25	  initialValue: ConnectionStateInitialValue = {},
26	): ConnectionStateContainer {
27	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
28	    ? cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(initialValue.snapshot)
29	    : createEmptyConnectionSnapshot();
30	
31	  return {
32	    getSnapshot() {
33	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
34	    },
35	
36	    replaceSnapshot(nextSnapshot) {
37	      snapshot = cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(nextSnapshot);
38	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
39	    },
40	
41	    upsertConfig(config) {
42	      snapshot = upsertConnectionConfig(snapshot, config);
43	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
44	    },
45	
46	    applyEvent(event) {
47	      snapshot = reduceTransportEvent(snapshot, event);
48	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
49	    },
50	
51	    resetSnapshot(nextSnapshot = createEmptyConnectionSnapshot()) {
52	      snapshot = cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(nextSnapshot);
53	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
54	    },
55	  };
56	}
57

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/adapters/real-local-material-adapter.ts",
  "content": "import type { StorageMaterialBucket } from '../core';\nimport type {\n  LocalMaterialAdapter,\n  LocalMaterialReadResult,\n  LocalMaterialWriteResult,\n  LocalMaterialDeleteResult,\n  LocalMaterialListResult,\n  LocalMaterialAdapterError,\n} from './ports';\nimport type { FileFacade } from '@/platform';\n\nexport interface RealLocalMaterialAdapterOptions {\n  readonly fileFacade: FileFacade;\n  readonly baseDir: string;\n}\n\nfunction filePath(baseDir: string, bucket: StorageMaterialBucket, id: string): string {\n  const safeId = id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '_');\n  return `${baseDir}/${bucket}/${safeId}.json`;\n}\n\nfunction dirPath(baseDir: string, bucket: StorageMaterialBucket): string {\n  return `${baseDir}/${bucket}`;\n}\n\nfunction adapterError(kind: LocalMaterialAdapterError['kind'], message: string): LocalMaterialAdapterError {\n  return { kind, message };\n}\n\nexport function createRealLocalMaterialAdapter(\n  options: RealLocalMaterialAdapterOptions,\n): LocalMaterialAdapter {\n  const { fileFacade, baseDir } = options;\n\n  return {\n    async readMaterial(bucket, id): Promise<LocalMaterialReadResult> {\n      try {\n        const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath(baseDir, bucket, id));\n        const value: unknown = JSON.parse(text);\n        return { ok: true, value };\n      } catch (err: unknown) {\n        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n        if (message.includes('ENOENT')) {\n          return { ok: false, error: adapterError('missing', `Material not found: ${bucket}/${id}`) };\n        }\n        if (message.includes('JSON') || message.includes('Unexpected')) {\n          console.error(`[storage] Corrupted material ${bucket}/${id}:`, message);\n          return { ok: false, error: adapterError('corrupted', `Corrupted material: ${bucket}/${id}`) };\n        }\n        return { ok: false, error: adapterError('unavailable', message) };\n      }\n    },\n\n    async writeMaterial(bucket, id, value): Promise<LocalMaterialWriteResult> {\n      try {\n        const text = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);\n        await fileFacade.writeTextFile(filePath(baseDir, bucket, id), text);\n        return { ok: true };\n      } catch (err: unknown) {\n        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n        return { ok: false, error: adapterError('write-failed', message) };\n      }\n    },\n\n    async deleteMaterial(bucket, id): Promise<LocalMaterialDeleteResult> {\n      try {\n        const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath(baseDir, bucket, id));\n        const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(text);\n        await fileFacade.writeTextFile(\n          filePath(baseDir, bucket, `${id}.deleted`),\n          JSON.stringify({ deletedAt: new Date().toISOString(), original: parsed }),\n        );\n        return { ok: true };\n      } catch (err: unknown) {\n        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n        if (message.includes('ENOENT')) {\n          return { ok: true };\n        }\n        return { ok: false, error: adapterError('unavailable', message) };\n      }\n    },\n\n    async listMaterials(bucket): Promise<LocalMaterialListResult> {\n      try {\n        const dir = dirPath(baseDir, bucket);\n        const indexText = await fileFacade.readTextFile(`${dir}/_index.json`);\n        const ids: unknown = JSON.parse(indexText);\n        if (!Array.isArray(ids)) {\n          return { ok: true, ids: [] };\n        }\n        return { ok: true, ids: ids.filter((v): v is string => typeof v === 'string') };\n      } catch (err: unknown) {\n        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n        if (message.includes('ENOENT')) {\n          return { ok: true, ids: [] };\n        }\n        return { ok: false, error: adapterError('unavailable', message) };\n      }\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

12	export interface SettingsStateContainer {

15	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlySettingsSnapshot): SettingsSnapshot;
16	}
17	
18	export function createSettingsState(
19	  initialValue: SettingsStateInitialValue = {},
20	): SettingsStateContainer {
21	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
22	    ? cloneSettingsSnapshot(initialValue.snapshot)
23	    : createDefaultSettingsSnapshot();
24	
25	  return {
26	    getSnapshot() {
27	      return cloneSettingsSnapshot(snapshot);
28	    },
29	
30	    replaceSnapshot(nextSnapshot) {

35	    resetSnapshot(nextSnapshot = createDefaultSettingsSnapshot()) {

38	    },
