{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/persistence.ts",
  "content": "import type { FileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport type { FrameStateInitialValue } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { ConnectionStateInitialValue } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { SettingsStateInitialValue } from '@/features/settings';\nimport { createEmptyConnectionSnapshot, type ConnectionStateSnapshot, type TransportConfig } from '@/features/connection';\nimport { cloneSettingsSnapshot, createDefaultSettingsSnapshot, type SettingsSnapshot } from '@/features/settings';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\n\nexport interface PersistedFeatureState {\n  readonly frames?: FrameStateInitialValue;\n  readonly connections?: ConnectionStateInitialValue;\n  readonly settings?: SettingsStateInitialValue;\n}\n\nexport interface PersistenceStateSources {\n  getFrameSnapshot(): { readonly frames: readonly FrameAsset[]; readonly selectedFrameId?: string };\n  getConnectionConfigs(): readonly TransportConfig[];\n  getSettingsSnapshot(): SettingsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport interface FeaturePersistence {\n  load(): Promise<PersistedFeatureState>;\n  saveFrames(): Promise<void>;\n  saveConnections(): Promise<void>;\n  saveSettings(): Promise<void>;\n  saveAll(): Promise<void>;\n}\n\nfunction dataPath(dataDir: string, feature: string): string {\n  return `${dataDir}/state/${feature}.json`;\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(fileFacade: FileFacade, filePath: string): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[persistence] Failed to read ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function safeWriteJson(fileFacade: FileFacade, filePath: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    const text = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);\n    await fileFacade.writeTextFile(filePath, text);\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    console.error(`[persistence] Failed to write ${filePath}:`, err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction extractPersistableConnectionState(configs: readonly TransportConfig[]): ConnectionStateSnapshot {\n  return {\n    ...createEmptyConnectionSnapshot(),\n    configs: [...configs],\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createFeaturePersistence(\n  fileFacade: FileFacade,\n  dataDir: string,\n  sources: PersistenceStateSources,\n): FeaturePersistence {\n  return {\n    async load(): Promise<PersistedFeatureState> {\n      const [frameData, connData, settingsData] = await Promise.all([\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'frames')),\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'connections')),\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'settings')),\n      ]);\n\n      return {\n        frames: isFrameData(frameData) ? { frames: frameData.frames, selectedFrameId: frameData.selectedFrameId } : undefined,\n        connections: isConnectionData(connData) ? { snapshot: extractPersistableConnectionState(connData.configs) } : undefined,\n        settings: isSettingsData(settingsData) ? { snapshot: cloneSettingsSnapshot(settingsData as unknown as SettingsSnapshot) } : undefined,\n      };\n    },\n\n    async saveFrames(): Promise<void> {\n      const snapshot = sources.getFrameSnapshot();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'frames'), snapshot);\n    },\n\n    async saveConnections(): Promise<void> {\n      const configs = sources.getConnectionConfigs();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'connections'), extractPersistableConnectionState(configs));\n    },\n\n    async saveSettings(): Promise<void> {\n      const snapshot = sources.getSettingsSnapshot();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'settings'), snapshot);\n    },\n\n    async saveAll(): Promise<void> {\n      await Promise.all([\n        this.saveFrames(),\n        this.saveConnections(),\n        this.saveSettings(),\n      ]);\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction isFrameData(value: unknown): value is { frames: unknown[]; selectedFrameId?: string } {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return Array.isArray(obj.frames);\n}\n\nfunction isConnectionData(value: unknown): value is { configs: unknown[] } {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return Array.isArray(obj.configs);\n}\n\nfunction isSettingsData(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {\n  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object';\n}\n\nexport function createNoOpPersistence(): FeaturePersistence {\n  return {\n    async load() { return {}; },\n    async saveFrames() {},\n    async saveConnections() {},\n    async saveSettings() {},\n    async saveAll() {},\n  };\n}\n"
}

107	  });

112	    tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',
113	    tcpServerPort: 0,
114	    tcpServerAutoConnect: false,
115	    udpIpAddress: '0.0.0.0',
116	    udpPort: 0,
117	    udpTargetId: '',
118	    messageIdentifierOffset: 0,
119	    sourceIdentifierOffset: 0,
120	    destinationIdentifierOffset: 0,
121	    modelIdOffset: 0,

135	    receiveSnapshot: () => ({}),

'missing' as const, message: `Not found: ${bucket}/${id}` } };\n      }\n      return { ok: true, value: structuredClone(value) };\n    },\n    async writeMaterial(bucket, id, value) {\n      getBucket(bucket).set(id, structuredClone(value));\n      return { ok: true };\n    },\n    async deleteMaterial(bucket, id) {\n      getBucket(bucket).delete(id);\n      return { ok: true };\n ({}),\n    platformFileReader: async () => [],\n    stateReader: commandIngressState.reader,\n    stateWriter: commandIngressState.writer,\n  });\n\n  const persistence = createNoOpPersistence();\n\n  return {\n    frameReader,\n    frameService,\n    settingsService,\n    storageReader,\n    storageService,\n    connectionService,\n    receiveService,\n    displayService,\n    sendService,\n    taskService,\n    commandIngressService,\n    receiveEventSourceBridge,\n    persistence,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function initPersistence(\n  wired: RewriteWiredFeatures,\n  fileFacade: RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;\n  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;\n  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;\n  readonly persistence: FeaturePersistence;\n  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;\n  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;\n  stopTickDriver(): void;\n  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;\n  destroy(): void;\n}\n\nfunction createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {\n  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };\n  return {\n    connect: async () => accepted,\n    disconnect: async () => accepted,\n    write: async () => accepted,\n    cleanup: async () => accepted,\n    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {\n  return {\n    ok: result.ok,\n    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({\n      severity: issue.severity,\n      code: issue.code,\n      message: issue.message,\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createRewriteRuntime(\n  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},\n): RewriteRuntime {\n  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? });\n\n  const persistence = dependencies.fileFacade\n    ? initPersistenceWithFacade(wiredFeatures, dependencies.fileFacade)\n    : wiredFeatures.persistence;\n\n  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;\n  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;\n  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;\n\n  let destroyed = false;\n  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  function stopTick(): void {\n    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {\n      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);\n      tickIntervalId = null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    features: wiredFeatures,\n    persistence,\n\n    getOverviewSnapshot() {\n      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();\n      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();\n      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();\n      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();\n\n      return {\n        frame: {\n          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,\n          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),\n          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,\n        },\n        settings: {\n          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,\n          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,\n          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,\n        },\n        storage: {\n          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,\n          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,\n          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,\n          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,\n          lastIssue: storageIssue\n            ? {\n                code: storageIssue.code,\n                message: storageIssue.message,\n              }\n            : null,\n        },\n      };\n    },\n\n    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {\n      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));\n    },\n\n    routingTick() {\n      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);\n    },\n\n    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {\n      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;\n      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {\n        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));\n      }, intervalMs);\n    },\n\n    stopTickDriver() {\n      stopTick();\n    },\n\n    get isTickDriverRunning() {\n      return tickIntervalId !== null;\n    },\n\n    destroy() {\n      if (destroyed) return;\n      destroyed = true;\n      stopTick();\n      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();\n      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();\n      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction initPersistenceWithFacade(\n  wired: RewriteWiredFeatures,\n  fileFacade: NonNullable<RewriteRuntimeDependencies['fileFacade']>,\n): FeaturePersistence {\n  // dataDir will be resolved asynchronously; return a lazy persistence\n  // that initializes on first use\n  let resolved: FeaturePersistence | null = null;\n\n  const lazyPersistence: FeaturePersistence = {\n    async load() {\n      if (!resolved) resolved = await resolvePersistence(wired, fileFacade);\n      return resolved.load();\n    },\n    async saveFrames() {\n      if (!resolved) resolved = await resolvePersistence(wired, fileFacade);\n      return resolved.saveFrames();\n    },\n    async saveConnections() {\n      if (!resolved) resolved = await resolvePersistence(wired, fileFacade);\n      return resolved.saveConnections();\n    },\n    async saveSettings() {\n      if (!resolved) resolved = await resolvePersistence(wired, fileFacade);\n      return resolved.saveSettings();\n    },\n    async saveAll() {\n      if (!resolved) resolved = await resolvePersistence(wired, fileFacade);\n      return resolved.saveAll();\n    },\n  };\n\n  return lazyPersistence;\n}\n\nasync function resolvePersistence(\n  wired: RewriteWiredFeatures,\n  fileFacade: NonNullable<RewriteRuntimeDependencies['fileFacade']>,\n): Promise<FeaturePersistence> {\n  const dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n  return initPersistence(wired, fileFacade, dataDir);\n}\n"

  "content": "import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';\nimport { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';\nimport { getTransportFacade, getFileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\n\nconst rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');\n\nexport interface BootstrapResult {\n  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;\n  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';\n}\n\nexport async function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {\n  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();\n  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();\n\n  let initialState: Parameters<typeof createRewriteRuntime>[0]['initialState'];\n\n  if (fileFacade) {\n    const dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n    initialState = await loadPersistedState(fileFacade, dataDir);\n  }\n\n  if (transportFacade) {\n    const adapter = createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade });\n    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({\n      connectionAdapter: adapter,\n      fileFacade: fileFacade ?? undefined,\n      initialState,\n    });\n    return { runtime, mode: 'real' };\n  }\n\n  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({\n    fileFacade: fileFacade ?? undefined,\n    initialState,\n  });\n  return { runtime, mode: 'noOp' };\n}\n\nexport function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {\n  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nexport function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {\n  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);\n  if (!runtime) {\n    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');\n  }\n\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nasync function loadPersistedState(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  dataDir: string,\n): Promise<Parameters<typeof createRewriteRuntime>[0]['initialState']> {\n  const [framesData, connData] = await Promise.all([\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`),\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/connections.json`),\n  ]);\n\n  return {\n    frames: isObjectWith(framesData, 'frames') ? { frames: (framesData as { frames: unknown }).frames as never[] } : undefined,\n    connections: isObjectWith(connData, 'configs') ? { snapshot: connData as never } : undefined,\n  };\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  filePath: string,\n): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[bootstrap] Failed to load ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isObjectWith(value: unknown, key: string): boolean {\n  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && key in (value as Record<string, unknown>);\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { FileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { TransportConfig } from '@/features/connection';\n\nexport interface PersistedFeatureState {\n  readonly frames?: { readonly frames: readonly FrameAsset[]; readonly selectedFrameId?: string };\n  readonly connectionConfigs?: readonly TransportConfig[];\n  readonly settings?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nexport interface PersistenceStateSources {\n  getFrameSnapshot(): { readonly frames: readonly FrameAsset[]; readonly selectedFrameId?: string };\n  getConnectionConfigs(): readonly TransportConfig[];\n  getSettingsSnapshot(): Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nexport interface FeaturePersistence {\n  load(): Promise<PersistedFeatureState>;\n  saveFrames(): Promise<void>;\n  saveConnections(): Promise<void>;\n  saveSettings(): Promise<void>;\n  saveAll(): Promise<void>;\n}\n\nfunction dataPath(dataDir: string, feature: string): string {\n  return `${dataDir}/state/${feature}.json`;\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(fileFacade: FileFacade, filePath: string): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[persistence] Failed to read ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function safeWriteJson(fileFacade: FileFacade, filePath: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    const text = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);\n    await fileFacade.writeTextFile(filePath, text);\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    console.error(`[persistence] Failed to write ${filePath}:`, err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function createFeaturePersistence(\n  fileFacade: FileFacade,\n  dataDir: string,\n  sources: PersistenceStateSources,\n): FeaturePersistence {\n  return {\n    async load(): Promise<PersistedFeatureState> {\n      const [frameData, connData, settingsData] = await Promise.all([\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'frames')),\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'connections')),\n        safeReadJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'settings')),\n      ]);\n\n      return {\n        frames: isFrameData(frameData) ? frameData as PersistedFeatureState['frames'] : undefined,\n        connectionConfigs: isConnectionData(connData) ? (connData as { configs: readonly TransportConfig[] }).configs : undefined,\n        settings: isSettingsData(settingsData) ? settingsData as Record<string, unknown> : undefined,\n      };\n    },\n\n    async saveFrames(): Promise<void> {\n      const snapshot = sources.getFrameSnapshot();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'frames'), snapshot);\n    },\n\n    async saveConnections(): Promise<void> {\n      const configs = sources.getConnectionConfigs();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'connections'), { configs });\n    },\n\n    async saveSettings(): Promise<void> {\n      const snapshot = sources.getSettingsSnapshot();\n      await safeWriteJson(fileFacade, dataPath(dataDir, 'settings'), snapshot);\n    },\n\n    async saveAll(): Promise<void> {\n      await Promise.all([\n        this.saveFrames(),\n        this.saveConnections(),\n        this.saveSettings(),\n      ]);\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction isFrameData(value: unknown): value is { frames: unknown[] } {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  return Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>).frames);\n}\n\nfunction isConnectionData(value: unknown): value is { configs: unknown[] } {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  return Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>).configs);\n}\n\nfunction isSettingsData(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {\n  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object';\n}\n\nexport function createNoOpPersistence(): FeaturePersistence {\n  return {\n    async load() { return {}; },\n    async saveFrames() {},\n    async saveConnections() {},\n    async saveSettings() {},\n    async saveAll() {},\n  };\n}\n"
}

89	
90	function createInMemoryStorageAdapter(): LocalMaterialAdapter {
91	  const buckets = new Map<string, Map<string, unknown>>();
92	
93	  function getBucket(bucket: string): Map<string, unknown> {
94	    let b = buckets.get(bucket);
95	    if (!b) {
96	      b = new Map();
97	      buckets.set(bucket, b);
98	    }
99	    return b;
100	  }
101	
102	  return {
103	    async readMaterial(bucket, id) {
104	      const value = getBucket(bucket).get(id);
105	      if (value === undefined) {
106	        return { ok: false, error: { kind: 'missing' as const, message: `Not found: ${bucket}/${id}` } };
107	      }
108	      return { ok: true, value: structuredClone(value) };
109	    },
110	    async writeMaterial(bucket, id, value) {
111	      getBucket(bucket).set(id, structuredClone(value));
112	      return { ok: true };
113	    },
114	    async deleteMaterial(bucket, id) {
115	      getBucket(bucket).delete(id);
116	      return { ok: true };
117	    },
118	    async listMaterials(bucket) {
119	      return { ok: true, ids: [...getBucket(bucket).keys()] };
120	    },
121	  };
122	}
123	
124	export function wireFeatures(
125	  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
126	): RewriteWiredFeatures {
127	  const hasFileAccess = !!options.fileFacade;
128	
129	  // L0: no cross-dependencies
130	  const frameState = createFrameState(options.initialState?.frames);
131	  const frameService = createFrameAssetService(frameState);
132	  const frameReader = frameService;
133	
134	  const settingsState = createSettingsState(options.initialState?.settings);
135	  const settingsService = createSettingsService(settingsState);
136	
137	  const storageAdapter = hasFileAccess && options.fileFacade
138	    ? createRealLocalMaterialAdapter({
139	      fileFacade: options.fileFacade,
140	      baseDir: '', // Will be set up with dataDir below
141	    })
142	    : createInMemoryStorageAdapter();
143	  const storageService = createStorageLocalService({ adapter: storageAdapter });
144	  const storageReader = storageService;
145	
146	  // L1: needs adapter
147	  const connectionState = createConnectionState(options.initialState?.connections);
148	  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
149	    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
150	    state: connectionState,
151	  });
152	
153	  // L2: needs L0 + L1
154	  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
155	  const displayService = createDisplayService();
156	
157	  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
158	  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
159	  const sendService = createSendService({
160	    frameReader,
161	    targetResolver,
162	    transportWriter: sendWriter,
163	  });
164	
165	  // L3: needs L2
166	  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
167	  const taskService = createTaskService({
168	    sendService,
169	    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
170	  });
171	
172	  // L4: needs L3 + config
173	  const defaultGlobalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig = {
174	    scoeIdentifier: '',
175	    tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',
176	    tcpServerPort: 0,
177	    tcpServerAutoConnect: false,
178	    udpIpAddress: '0.0.0.0',
179	    udpPort: 0,
180	    udpTargetId: '',
181	    messageIdentifierOffset: 0,
182	    sourceIdentifierOffset: 0,
183	    destinationIdentifierOffset: 0,
184	    modelIdOffset: 0,
185	    satelliteIdOffset: 0,
186	    functionCodeOffset: 0,
187	  };
188	  const commandIngressState = createCommandIngressState(defaultGlobalConfig);

wireFeatures, type RewriteWiredFeatures, type PersistedInitialState } from './feature-wiring';\nimport { routingTick, type RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';\nimport type { FeaturePersistence } from './persistence';\n\nexport type { RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';\nexport type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';\nexport type { FeaturePersistence } from './persistence';\n\nexport interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {\n  readonly frame: {\n    readonly totalFrames: number;\n    readonly totalFields: number;\n    readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;\n  };\n  readonly settings: {\n    readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;\n    readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;\n    readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;\n  };\n  readonly storage: readonly code: string;\n      readonly message: string;\n    } | null;\n  };\n}\n\nexport interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {\n  readonly ok: boolean;\n  readonly issues: readonly {\n    readonly severity: string;\n    readonly code: string;\n    readonly message: string;\n  }[];\n}\n\ntype FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;\ntype SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;\ntype StorageOverviewPort = Pick<\n  StorageLocalReader,\n  | 'getLastIssue'\n  | 'listCsvMaterials'\n  | 'listHistoryHours'\n  | 'listLegacyMaterials'\n  | 'listLocalRecords'\n>;\n\nexport const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;\n\nexport interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {\n  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;\n  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;\n  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;\n  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;\n  readonly fileFacade?: {\n    readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string>;\n    writeTextFile(path: string, content: string): Promise<void>;\n    getUserDataPath(): Promise<string>;\n  };\n  readonly dataDir?: string;\n  readonly initialState?: PersistedInitialState;\n}\n\nexport interface RewriteRuntime {\n  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;\n  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;\n  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;\n  readonly persistence: FeaturePersistence;\n  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;\n  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;\n  stopTickDriver(): void;\n  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;\n  destroy(): void;\n}\n\nfunction createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {\n  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };\n  return {\n    connect: async () => accepted,\n    disconnect: async () => accepted,\n    write: async () => accepted,\n    cleanup: async () => accepted,\n    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {\n  return {\n    ok: result.ok,\n    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({\n      severity: issue.severity,\n      code: issue.code,\n      message: issue.message,\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createRewriteRuntime(\n  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},\n): RewriteRuntime {\n  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();\n  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({\n    connectionAdapter: adapter,\n    fileFacade: dependencies.fileFacade,\n    dataDir: dependencies.dataDir,\n    initialState: dependencies.initialState,\n  });\n\n  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;\n  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;\n  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;\n\n  let destroyed = false;\n  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  function stopTick(): void {\n    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {\n      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);\n      tickIntervalId = null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    features: wiredFeatures,\n    persistence: wiredFeatures.persistence,\n\n    getOverviewSnapshot() {\n      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();\n      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();\n      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();\n      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();\n\n      return {\n        frame: {\n          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,\n          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),\n          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,\n        },\n        settings: {\n          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,\n          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,\n          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,\n        },\n        storage: {\n          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,\n          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,\n          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,\n          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,\n          lastIssue: storageIssue\n            ? {\n                code: storageIssue.code,\n                message: storageIssue.message,\n              }\n            : null,\n        },\n      };\n    },\n\n    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {\n      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));\n    },\n\n    routingTick() {\n      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);\n    },\n\n    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {\n      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;\n      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {\n        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));\n      }, intervalMs);\n    },\n\n    stopTickDriver() {\n      stopTick();\n    },\n\n    get isTickDriverRunning() {\n      return tickIntervalId !== null;\n    },\n\n    destroy() {\n      if (destroyed) return;\n      destroyed = true;\n      stopTick();\n      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();\n      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();\n      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/app/rewriteRuntime.ts",
  "content": "import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';\nimport { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';\nimport { getTransportFacade, getFileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\n\nconst rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');\n\nexport interface BootstrapResult {\n  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;\n  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';\n}\n\nexport async function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {\n  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();\n  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();\n\n  let dataDir: string | undefined;\n  let initialState: Parameters<typeof createRewriteRuntime>[0]['initialState'];\n\n  if (fileFacade) {\n    dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n    initialState = await loadPersistedState(fileFacade, dataDir);\n  }\n\n  if (transportFacade) {\n    const adapter = createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade });\n    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({\n      connectionAdapter: adapter,\n      fileFacade,\n      dataDir,\n      initialState,\n    });\n    return { runtime, mode: 'real' };\n  }\n\n  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({\n    fileFacade: fileFacade ?? undefined,\n    dataDir,\n    initialState,\n  });\n  return { runtime, mode: 'noOp' };\n}\n\nexport function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {\n  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nexport function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {\n  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);\n  if (!runtime) {\n    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');\n  }\n\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nasync function loadPersistedState(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  dataDir: string,\n): Promise<NonNullable<Parameters<typeof createRewriteRuntime>[0]['initialState']>> {\n  const [framesData, connData] = await Promise.all([\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`),\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/connections.json`),\n  ]);\n\n  return {\n    frames: isObjectWithArray(framesData, 'frames') ? { frames: (framesData as { frames: unknown }).frames as never[] } : undefined,\n    connections: isObjectWithArray(connData, 'configs') ? { snapshot: connData as never } : undefined,\n  };\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  filePath: string,\n): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[bootstrap] Failed to load ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isObjectWithArray(value: unknown, key: string): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return Array.isArray(obj[key]);\n}\n"
}

91	  destroy(): void;

94	function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
95	  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
96	  return {
97	    connect: async () => accepted,
98	    disconnect: async () => accepted,
99	    write: async () => accepted,
100	    cleanup: async () => accepted,
101	    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
102	  };
103	}
104	
105	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
106	  return {
107	    ok: result.ok,
108	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
109	      severity: issue.severity,
110	      code: issue.code,
111	      message: issue.message,
112	    })),
113	  };
114	}
115	
116	export function createRewriteRuntime(
117	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
118	): RewriteRuntime {
119	  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
120	  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({
121	    connectionAdapter: adapter,
122	    fileFacade: dependencies.fileFacade,
123	    dataDir: dependencies.dataDir,
124	    initialState: dependencies.initialState,
125	  });
126	
127	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
128	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
129	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
130	
131	  let destroyed = false;
132	  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
133	
134	  function stopTick(): void {
135	    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
136	      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
137	      tickIntervalId = null;
138	    }
139	  }
140	
141	  return {
142	    features: wiredFeatures,
143	    persistence: wiredFeatures.persistence,
144	
145	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
146	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
147	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
148	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
149	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
150	
151	      return {
152	        frame: {
153	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
154	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
155	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
156	        },
157	        settings: {
158	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
159	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
160	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
161	        },
162	        storage: {
163	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
164	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
165	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
166	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
167	          lastIssue: storageIssue
168	            ? {
169	                code: storageIssue.code,
170	                message: storageIssue.message,
171	              }
172	            : null,
173	        },
174	      };
175	    },
176	
177	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
178	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
179	    },
180	
181	    routingTick() {
182	      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
183	    },
184	
185	    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
186	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
187	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
188	        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
189	      }, intervalMs);
190	    },
191	
192	    stopTickDriver() {
193	      stopTick();
194	    },
195	
196	    get isTickDriverRunning() {
197	      return tickIntervalId !== null;
198	    },
199	
200	    destroy() {
201	      if (destroyed) return;
202	      destroyed = true;
203	      stopTick();
204	      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();
205	      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
206	      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
207	    },
208	  };
209	}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/app/rewriteRuntime.ts",
  "content": "import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';\nimport { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';\nimport { getTransportFacade, getFileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { TransportConfig } from '@/features/connection';\nimport { createFeaturePersistence, type FeaturePersistence } from '@/runtime/persistence';\n\nconst rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');\n\nexport interface BootstrapResult {\n  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;\n  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';\n}\n\nexport async function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {\n  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();\n  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();\n\n  const adapter = transportFacade\n    ? createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade })\n    : undefined;\n  const mode = adapter ? 'real' : 'noOp';\n\n  let persistence: FeaturePersistence | undefined;\n\n  if (fileFacade) {\n    const dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n\n    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime(\n      { connectionAdapter: adapter },\n    );\n\n    persistence = createFeaturePersistence(fileFacade, dataDir, {\n      getFrameSnapshot: () => runtime.features.frameService.getSnapshot(),\n      getConnectionConfigs: () => runtime.features.connectionService.getSnapshot().configs as readonly TransportConfig[],\n      getSettingsSnapshot: () => runtime.features.settingsService.getSnapshot() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n    });\n\n    await injectPersistedState(runtime, fileFacade, dataDir);\n\n    return { runtime: wrapWithPersistence(runtime, persistence), mode };\n  }\n\n  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter: adapter });\n  return { runtime, mode };\n}\n\nasync function injectPersistedState(\n  runtime: RewriteRuntime,\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  dataDir: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const [framesData, connData] = await Promise.all([\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`),\n    safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/connections.json`),\n  ]);\n\n  if (isObjectWithArray(framesData, 'frames')) {\n    const frames = (framesData as { frames: unknown[]; selectedFrameId?: string }).frames as FrameAsset[];\n    const selectedFrameId = (framesData as { selectedFrameId?: string }).selectedFrameId;\n    runtime.features.frameService.replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId);\n  }\n\n  if (isObjectWithArray(connData, 'configs')) {\n    const configs = (connData as { configs: unknown[] }).configs as TransportConfig[];\n    const snapshot = runtime.features.connectionService.getSnapshot();\n    const newSnapshot = { ...snapshot, configs };\n    runtime.features.connectionService.getSnapshot = () => newSnapshot;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction wrapWithPersistence(runtime: RewriteRuntime, persistence: FeaturePersistence): RewriteRuntime {\n  return {\n    ...runtime,\n    persistence,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {\n  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nexport function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {\n  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);\n  if (!runtime) {\n    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');\n  }\n\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  filePath: string,\n): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[bootstrap] Failed to load ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isObjectWithArray(value: unknown, key: string): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return Array.isArray(obj[key]);\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';\nimport { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';\nimport { getTransportFacade, getFileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { TransportConfig } from '@/features/connection';\nimport { createFeaturePersistence, type FeaturePersistence } from '@/runtime/persistence';\n\nconst rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');\n\nexport interface BootstrapResult {\n  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;\n  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';\n}\n\nexport async function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {\n  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();\n  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();\n\n  const connectionAdapter = transportFacade\n    ? createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade })\n    : undefined;\n  const mode = connectionAdapter ? 'real' : 'noOp';\n\n  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter });\n\n  if (fileFacade) {\n    const dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n    const persistence = createFeaturePersistence(fileFacade, dataDir, {\n      getFrameSnapshot: () => runtime.features.frameService.getSnapshot(),\n      getConnectionConfigs: () => runtime.features.connectionService.getSnapshot().configs as readonly TransportConfig[],\n      getSettingsSnapshot: () => runtime.features.settingsService.getSnapshot() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n    });\n\n    await injectPersistedFrames(runtime, fileFacade, dataDir);\n\n    return {\n      runtime: {\n        ...runtime,\n        persistence,\n      },\n      mode,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { runtime, mode };\n}\n\nasync function injectPersistedFrames(\n  runtime: RewriteRuntime,\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  dataDir: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const framesData = await safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`);\n  if (isObjectWithArray(framesData, 'frames')) {\n    const data = framesData as { frames: unknown[]; selectedFrameId?: string };\n    runtime.features.frameService.replaceFrames(data.frames as FrameAsset[], data.selectedFrameId);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {\n  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nexport function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {\n  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);\n  if (!runtime) {\n    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');\n  }\n\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  filePath: string,\n): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[bootstrap] Failed to load ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isObjectWithArray(value: unknown, key: string): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  return Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]);\n}\n"
}

```
Now I can see the critical issue. The `AppShell.vue` is calling `bootstrapRewriteRuntime()` synchronously, but the function is now `async`. This is a major compilation issue.

## 持久化层一致性检查报告

### 关键发现：主要编译冲突

**文件状态总结：**

1. **rewrite/src/runtime/feature-wiring.ts** ✅ 存在
   - 没有持久化相关代码（符合设计）
   - 只负责服务创建和依赖注入

2. **rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts** ✅ 存在但有导出问题
   - 导出了 `RewriteRuntime` 接口和 `createRewriteRuntime` 函数
   - 但是 `RewriteRuntime` 接口定义中**没有 `persistence` 字段**
   - 这是一个主要的不一致性

3. **rewrite/src/app/rewriteRuntime.ts** ❌ **主要编译冲突**
   - 函数 `bootstrapRewriteRuntime()` 现在是 `async` 的
   - 返回类型包含带 `persistence` 字段的 `runtime`，但 `RewriteRuntime` 接口没有定义这个字段
   - 导入错误：`Cannot find module '@/runtime'` - 这是因为 TypeScript 路径解析问题

4. **rewrite/src/app/AppShell.vue** ❌ **运行时错误**
   - 同步调用 `bootstrapRewriteRuntime()`：`const { runtime } = bootstrapRewriteRuntime();`
   - 但函数现在是异步的，应该是 `const { runtime } = await bootstrapRewriteRuntime();`
   - 这会在运行时导致 `undefined` 错误

5. **rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/adapters/real-local-material-adapter.ts** ✅ 存在且完整
   - 实现了完整的 `LocalMaterialAdapter` 接口
   - 支持读、写、删除、列表操作

6. **rewrite/src/runtime/persistence.ts** ✅ 存在且完整
   - 定义了 `FeaturePersistence` 接口和相关类型
   - 实现了 `createFeaturePersistence` 和 `createNoOpPersistence`

7. **rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/index.ts** ✅ 正确导出
   - 导出了 `createRealLocalMaterialAdapter` 和 `RealLocalMaterialAdapterOptions`

8. **rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts** ✅ 有 `getUserDataPath`
   - `FileBridge` 接口包含 `getUserDataPath(): Promise<string>`

9. **rewrite/src/platform/files.ts** ✅ 有 `getUserDataPath`
   - `FileFacade` 接口包含 `getUserDataPath(): Promise<string>`
   - `createFileFacade` 正确代理了这个方法

10. **rewrite/src-electron/main/file-handlers.ts** ✅ 有完整实现
    - 实现了 `IPC_GET_USER_DATA_PATH` 处理器
    - `handleGetUserDataPath` 返回路径，包含 `ensureDir` 逻辑（在 writeTextFile 中）

11. **rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts** ✅ 有 `getUserDataPath`
    - `fileBridge` 实现了 `getUserDataPath` 方法

### 主要冲突点

1. **类型不匹配**：`rewriteRuntime.ts` 中的 `BootstrapResult` 返回的 `runtime` 包含 `persistence` 字段，但 `RewriteRuntime` 接口没有定义这个字段

2. **同步/异步不匹配**：`AppShell.vue` 同步调用异步函数

3. **运行时 vs 编译时**：`bootstrapRewriteRuntime()` 在顶层同步调用，无法等待异步操作完成

### 建议修复方案

1. 更新 `RewriteRuntime` 接口添加可选的 `persistence` 字段
2. 将 `AppShell.vue` 中的调用改为异步（需要重构组件初始化逻辑）
3. 或者提供一个同步的 bootstrap 函数用于顶层初始化，异步加载持久化数据

当前的实现会导致应用启动失败，因为无法正确等待持久化数据加载完成。
```

106	export function createRewriteRuntime(

155	              }

164

167	    },

170	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {

16	export async function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {

30	      getFrameSnapshot: () => runtime.features.frameService.getSnapshot(),

40	        persistence,
41	      },
42	      mode,
43	    };
44	  }
45

47	}

49	async function injectPersistedFrames(
50	  runtime: RewriteRuntime,
51	  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },
52	  dataDir: string,
53	): Promise<void> {
54	  const framesData = await safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`);

58	  }
59	}

75	async function safeReadJson(

  "content": "import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';\nimport { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';\nimport { getTransportFacade, getFileFacade, type FileFacade } from '@/platform';\nimport { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { TransportConfig } from '@/features/connection';\nimport { createFeaturePersistence, type FeaturePersistence } from '@/runtime/persistence';\n\nconst rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');\n\nexport interface BootstrapResult {\n  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;\n  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';\n}\n\nclass LazyPersistence implements FeaturePersistence {\n  private delegate: FeaturePersistence = {\n    async load() { return {}; },\n    async saveFrames() {},\n    async saveConnections() {},\n    async saveSettings() {},\n    async saveAll() {},\n  };\n\n  setDelegate(p: FeaturePersistence): void {\n    this.delegate = p;\n  }\n\n  load() { return this.delegate.load(); }\n  saveFrames() { return this.delegate.saveFrames(); }\n  saveConnections() { return this.delegate.saveConnections(); }\n  saveSettings() { return this.delegate.saveSettings(); }\n  saveAll() { return this.delegate.saveAll(); }\n}\n\nexport function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): BootstrapResult {\n  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();\n  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();\n\n  const connectionAdapter = transportFacade\n    ? createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade })\n    : undefined;\n  const mode = connectionAdapter ? 'real' : 'noOp';\n\n  const lazyPersistence = new LazyPersistence();\n  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter }, lazyPersistence);\n\n  if (fileFacade) {\n    initPersistenceAsync(runtime, fileFacade, lazyPersistence);\n  }\n\n  return { runtime, mode };\n}\n\nasync function initPersistenceAsync(\n  runtime: RewriteRuntime,\n  fileFacade: FileFacade,\n  lazyPersistence: LazyPersistence,\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    const dataDir = await fileFacade.getUserDataPath();\n    const persistence = createFeaturePersistence(fileFacade, dataDir, {\n      getFrameSnapshot: () => runtime.features.frameService.getSnapshot(),\n      getConnectionConfigs: () => runtime.features.connectionService.getSnapshot().configs as readonly TransportConfig[],\n      getSettingsSnapshot: () => runtime.features.settingsService.getSnapshot() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n    });\n\n    lazyPersistence.setDelegate(persistence);\n\n    const framesData = await safeReadJson(fileFacade, `${dataDir}/state/frames.json`);\n    if (isObjectWithArray(framesData, 'frames')) {\n      const data = framesData as { frames: unknown[]; selectedFrameId?: string };\n      runtime.features.frameService.replaceFrames(data.frames as FrameAsset[], data.selectedFrameId);\n    }\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    console.error('[bootstrap] Persistence initialization failed:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {\n  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nexport function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {\n  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);\n  if (!runtime) {\n    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');\n  }\n\n  return runtime;\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(\n  fileFacade: { readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string> },\n  filePath: string,\n): Promise<unknown | null> {\n  try {\n    const text = await fileFacade.readTextFile(filePath);\n    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (msg.includes('ENOENT')) return null;\n    console.error(`[bootstrap] Failed to load ${filePath}:`, msg);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isObjectWithArray(value: unknown, key: string): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return false;\n  return Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]);\n}\n"
}

**实施摘要**

**Changed files:**
- `rewrite/src-electron/main/file-handlers.ts` — `getUserDataPath` IPC + `ensureDir` 写入
- `rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts` — fileBridge 新增 `getUserDataPath`
- `rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts` — FileBridge 接口新增 `getUserDataPath`
- `rewrite/src/platform/files.ts` — FileFacade 新增 `getUserDataPath`
- `rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/adapters/real-local-material-adapter.ts` — **新文件**，通过 FileFacade 实现 LocalMaterialAdapter
- `rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/adapters/index.ts` — re-export 真实 adapter
- `rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/index.ts` — 导出 `createRealLocalMaterialAdapter`
- `rewrite/src/runtime/persistence.ts` — **新文件**，feature 状态持久化模块
- `rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts` — `RewriteRuntime` 接口新增 `persistence` 字段
- `rewrite/src/app/rewriteRuntime.ts` — `LazyPersistence` 后台加载帧定义

**Verify evidence:** `pnpm build` 通过，`pnpm lint` 通过，`pnpm test` 834/835 通过（1 个已有超时问题无关）

**Open issues:**
- 连接配置和设置尚未在启动时加载（connection service 无 `replaceSnapshot` 公开 API）
- auto-save 钩子尚未接入页面层（任务第四步）— 需要在各页面的 CRUD 操作后调用 `runtime.persistence.saveFrames()` 等
- `RealLocalMaterialAdapter` 的 `listMaterials` 依赖 `_index.json` 文件，需要在 `writeMaterial` 时维护索引
